<script>
/* ==========================================================================
   调查局档案系统 — 单文件运行时
   分区：0 工具 1 应用数据 2 全局进度 3 路由视图 4 案件注册表 5 游戏引擎 6 启动
   ========================================================================== */
'use strict';
(function(){

/* ============================== 0. 工具层 ============================== */
const $  = (s, r) => (r || document).querySelector(s);
const $$ = (s, r) => Array.prototype.slice.call((r || document).querySelectorAll(s));
const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
const esc = s => String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const uid = (p) => (p || 'id') + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8) + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4);
const pad2 = n => (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n;
const todayStr = (d) => { const t = d || new Date(); return t.getFullYear() + '-' + pad2(t.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad2(t.getDate()); };
function greetWord(){ const h = new Date().getHours(); if (h < 6) return '凌晨好'; if (h < 11) return '早上好'; if (h < 14) return '午间好'; if (h < 18) return '下午好'; return '晚上好'; }

/* 安全存储：隐私模式 / 配额异常时不致命 */
const LS = {
  get(k, def){
    try { const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(k); if (raw == null) return def; return JSON.parse(raw); }
    catch (e) { return def; }
  },
  set(k, v){
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v)); return true; }
    catch (e) { toast('本地存储写入失败（隐私模式或空间已满）', 'red', '⚠'); return false; }
  },
  del(k){ try { window.localStorage.removeItem(k); return true; } catch (e) { return false; } }
};

/* localStorage 关键 key —— 与既有版本保持完全兼容 */
const KEYS = {
  profile : 'bureau-profile-v1',
  progress: 'bureau-global-progress-v1',
  custom  : 'bureau-custom-cases-v1',
  settings: 'bureau-settings-v1',
  save  : (caseId, slot) => 'case_' + caseId + '_save_' + slot,
  auto  : (caseId) => 'case_' + caseId + '_autosave',
  gallery: (caseId) => 'case_' + caseId + '_gallery'
};

/* ---------------------------- 音效（纯合成，无外链） ---------------------------- */
const SFX = {
  ctx: null,
  on: true,
  init(){
    if (this.ctx) return this.ctx;
    const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AC) return null;
    try { this.ctx = new AC(); } catch (e) { this.ctx = null; }
    return this.ctx;
  },
  /* 用振荡器合成短音：cues 为 [频率, 时长, 波形, 音量] */
  play(name){
    if (!this.on) return;
    try { this._play(name); } catch (e) { /* 浏览器音频策略/设备缺失时静默失败 */ }
  },
  _play(name){
    const map = {
      click   : [[392, .05, 'square', .035]],
      pick    : [[523, .06, 'triangle', .05], [784, .09, 'triangle', .05]],
      ok      : [[659, .07, 'sine', .055], [988, .12, 'sine', .05]],
      bad     : [[220, .16, 'sawtooth', .05], [146, .22, 'sawtooth', .045]],
      scare   : [[110, .3, 'sawtooth', .05], [82, .38, 'square', .04]],
      unlock  : [[523, .09, 'sine', .05], [659, .09, 'sine', .05], [880, .22, 'sine', .05]],
      page    : [[300, .04, 'triangle', .03]],
      achieve : [[784, .1, 'triangle', .05], [1046, .1, 'triangle', .05], [1318, .26, 'triangle', .045]],
      type    : [[1200, .012, 'square', .012]],
      modal   : [[330, .06, 'sine', .04], [494, .08, 'sine', .035]]
    };
    const cues = map[name];
    if (!cues) return;
    const ctx = this.init();
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended' && ctx.resume) { try { ctx.resume(); } catch (e) {} }
    let t = ctx.currentTime;
    cues.forEach(c => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator(), gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = c[2]; osc.frequency.value = c[0];
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(c[3], t + 0.012);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + c[1]);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(t); osc.stop(t + c[1] + .02);
      t += c[1] * .72;
    });
  },
  set(on){ this.on = !!on; if (on) this.play('modal'); }
};

/* ---------------------------- 提示条 ---------------------------- */
function toast(text, kind, icon, ms){
  const root = $('#toast-root');
  if (!root) return;
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'toast ' + (kind || '');
  el.innerHTML = '<span class="ti" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(icon || 'ℹ') + '</span><span class="tt">' + esc(text) + '</span>';
  root.appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => { el.classList.add('out'); setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 320); }, ms || (kind === 'gold' ? 4200 : 2600));
}

/* ---------------------------- 应用层弹窗 ---------------------------- */
let appModalEscHandler = null;
function closeAppModal(){
  const root = $('#app-modal-root');
  if (!root) return;
  root.classList.add('hidden');
  root.innerHTML = '';
  if (appModalEscHandler) { document.removeEventListener('keydown', appModalEscHandler, true); appModalEscHandler = null; }
}
function appModal(opt){
  const root = $('#app-modal-root');
  if (!root) return null;
  const o = opt || {};
  root.classList.remove('hidden');
  root.innerHTML =
    '<div class="modal ' + (o.wide ? 'wide' : '') + '" role="document">' +
      '<div class="modal-head"><h3>' + esc(o.title || '提示') + '</h3>' +
        '<button class="x-btn" data-act="close" aria-label="关闭">✕</button></div>' +
      '<div class="modal-body">' + (o.body || '') + '</div>' +
      (o.foot === null ? '' : '<div class="modal-foot">' + (o.foot || '<button class="btn primary" data-act="close">知道了</button>') + '</div>') +
    '</div>';
  root.onclick = (ev) => {
    const t = ev.target.closest('[data-act]');
    if (!t) { if (ev.target === root) closeAppModal(); return; }
    const act = t.getAttribute('data-act');
    if (act === 'close') { closeAppModal(); return; }
    if (o.onAction) o.onAction(act, t);
  };
  SFX.play('modal');
  appModalEscHandler = (ev) => { if (ev.key === 'Escape') { ev.stopPropagation(); closeAppModal(); } };
  document.addEventListener('keydown', appModalEscHandler, true);
  setTimeout(() => { const f = root.querySelector('input,textarea,select,.btn'); if (f) f.focus(); }, 40);
  return root.querySelector('.modal');
}
function confirmModal(title, text, onYes, yesLabel){
  appModal({
    title: title,
    body: '<p style="margin:0;color:var(--ink-2);white-space:pre-wrap">' + esc(text) + '</p>',
    foot: '<button class="btn ghost" data-act="close">取消</button>' +
          '<button class="btn danger" data-act="yes">' + esc(yesLabel || '确认') + '</button>',
    onAction: (a) => { if (a === 'yes') { closeAppModal(); if (onYes) onYes(); } }
  });
}

/* ============================== 1. 应用层数据 ============================== */

/* 内联 SVG 头像：不依赖任何外部资源 */
function makeAvatar(seed){
  const P = [
    ['#2b3446','#e2b04a'], ['#243043','#4ad2e6'], ['#31283a','#c08ce0'],
    ['#243830','#5ed08c'], ['#3a2a2a','#e0605f'], ['#2a3540','#dbe3ee']
  ][seed % 6];
  const g = [
    '<circle cx="40" cy="32" r="13"/>',
    '<rect x="26" y="20" width="28" height="24" rx="6"/>',
    '<path d="M40 18l12 22H28z"/>',
    '<path d="M28 30h24v18H28z"/>',
    '<circle cx="40" cy="30" r="11"/><rect x="26" y="42" width="28" height="10" rx="4"/>',
    '<path d="M40 16l10 14-10 22-10-22z"/>'
  ][seed % 6];
  const svg = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 80 80">' +
    '<rect width="80" height="80" fill="' + P[0] + '"/>' +
    '<g fill="none" stroke="' + P[1] + '" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.92">' + g + '</g>' +
    '<rect x="1" y="1" width="78" height="78" fill="none" stroke="' + P[1] + '" stroke-width="1" opacity="0.5"/></svg>';
  return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(svg);
}
const AVATARS = [0,1,2,3,4,5].map(makeAvatar);

const CASE_ARCHIVES = ARCHIVES.filter(a => a.type === 'case');

/* ============================== 2. 全局进度 ============================== */
const DEFAULT_PROGRESS = {
  version: 1,
  records: {},          /* caseId -> { completed, trueEnded, endings:{id:{ts,grade}}, playMs, evidence, lastAt } */
  unlocked: [],         /* 已解锁的隐藏档案 id */
  playMs: 0,            /* 累计游玩时长（毫秒） */
  evidenceTotal: 0,     /* 累计收集证据数 */
  secretRead: []        /* 已读隐藏档案 id */
};
function getGlobalProgress(){
  const raw = LS.get(KEYS.progress, null);
  const p = Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_PROGRESS, raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {});
  p.records = Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_PROGRESS.records, p.records || {});
  p.unlocked = Array.isArray(p.unlocked) ? p.unlocked : [];
  p.secretRead = Array.isArray(p.secretRead) ? p.secretRead : [];
  return p;
}
function saveGlobalProgress(p){ return LS.set(KEYS.progress, p); }

/* 图鉴（每案独立 key，兼容既有数据） */
function getGallery(caseId){ const g = LS.get(KEYS.gallery(caseId), {}); return g && typeof g === 'object' ? g : {}; }
function addToGallery(caseId, endingId, grade){
  const g = getGallery(caseId);
  if (!g[endingId]) { g[endingId] = { ts: Date.now(), grade: grade || '' }; LS.set(KEYS.gallery(caseId), g); return true; }
  return false;
}

/* 解锁条件判定：{kind:'true'|'allTrue', caseId/cases} */
function isArchiveUnlocked(arch, prog){
  if (!arch.hidden) return true;
  const p = prog || getGlobalProgress();
  if (arch.unlock && arch.unlock.kind === 'true') return !!(p.records[arch.unlock.caseId] && p.records[arch.unlock.caseId].trueEnded);
  if (arch.unlock && arch.unlock.kind === 'allTrue') return arch.unlock.cases.every(c => p.records[c] && p.records[c].trueEnded);
  return p.unlocked.indexOf(arch.id) >= 0;
}
function unlockableArchiveIds(prog){
  const p = prog || getGlobalProgress();
  return ARCHIVES.filter(a => a.hidden && isArchiveUnlocked(a, p)).map(a => a.id);
}
/* 结案登记：写入全局进度，返回本次新解锁的隐藏档案列表 */
function recordCaseCompleted(caseId, endingId, meta){
  const prog = getGlobalProgress();
  const m = meta || {};
  const rec = prog.records[caseId] || { completed:0, trueEnded:false, endings:{}, playMs:0, evidence:0, lastAt:0 };
  const isTrue = !!(m.isTrue);
  const wasTrue = !!rec.trueEnded;
  rec.completed = (rec.completed || 0) + 1;
  rec.endings = rec.endings || {};
  rec.endings[endingId] = { ts: Date.now(), grade: m.grade || '' };
  rec.trueEnded = wasTrue || isTrue;
  rec.playMs = (rec.playMs || 0) + (m.playMs || 0);
  rec.evidence = Math.max(rec.evidence || 0, m.evidence || 0);
  rec.lastAt = Date.now();
  prog.records[caseId] = rec;
  prog.playMs = (prog.playMs || 0) + (m.playMs || 0);
  prog.evidenceTotal = (prog.evidenceTotal || 0) + (m.evidence || 0);

  const before = prog.unlocked.slice();
  const newly = ARCHIVES.filter(a => a.hidden && isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog) && before.indexOf(a.id) < 0);
  const ids = ARCHIVES.filter(a => a.hidden && isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog)).map(a => a.id);
  prog.unlocked = ids;
  saveGlobalProgress(prog);
  return newly;
}
/* 案件状态：open / progress / clear / true */
function caseStatus(caseId){
  const prog = getGlobalProgress();
  const rec = prog.records[caseId];
  if (rec && rec.trueEnded) return 'true';
  if (rec && rec.completed > 0) return 'clear';
  let touched = false;
  for (let s = 1; s <= 3; s++) if (LS.get(KEYS.save(caseId, s), null)) touched = true;
  if (LS.get(KEYS.auto(caseId), null)) touched = true;
  if (getGallery(caseId) && Object.keys(getGallery(caseId)).length) touched = true;
  return touched ? 'progress' : 'open';
}
function fmtDuration(ms){
  if (!ms) return '0 分';
  const m = Math.floor(ms / 60000);
  if (m < 60) return m + ' 分';
  return Math.floor(m / 60) + ' 时 ' + (m % 60) + ' 分';
}
/* ============================== 2.5 个人信息 / 设置 / 自定义案件 ============================== */
function getProfile(){ const p = LS.get(KEYS.profile, null); return Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_PROFILE, p && typeof p === 'object' ? p : {}); }
function saveProfile(p){ return LS.set(KEYS.profile, Object.assign(getProfile(), p)); }

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = { sfx:true, typewriter:true, autosave:true, hints:true, lastCase:'' };
function getSettings(){ const s = LS.get(KEYS.settings, null); return Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, s && typeof s === 'object' ? s : {}); }
function saveSettings(patch){ const s = Object.assign(getSettings(), patch); LS.set(KEYS.settings, s); if ('sfx' in patch) SFX.set(!!patch.sfx); return s; }

const CUSTOM_ARCHIVES = [];
function allArchives(){ return ARCHIVES.concat(CUSTOM_ARCHIVES); }
function archiveById(id){ return allArchives().filter(a => a.id === id)[0] || null; }
function customCaseStore(){ const s = LS.get(KEYS.custom, null); return s && typeof s === 'object' ? s : {}; }
function loadCustomCases(){
  const store = customCaseStore();
  Object.keys(store).forEach(id => {
    const c = store[id];
    if (!c || typeof c !== 'object') return;
    try {
      CASES[id] = c;
      if (!archiveById(id)) {
        CUSTOM_ARCHIVES.push({
          id: id, type:'case', caseId:id, hidden:false, custom:true,
          title: c.title || ('自定义案件 ' + id), subtitle: c.subtitle || '用户导入卷宗',
          dept:'案件工坊', year: c.year || '——', place: c.place || '未知', tags: c.tags || ['自定义'],
          brief: c.brief || (c.storyIntro || '').slice(0, 80) || '由案件工坊导入的卷宗。',
          body: c.archiveBody || c.storyIntro || '（导入卷宗，无独立档案正文。）'
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* 单条损坏不影响整体 */ }
  });
}
function saveCustomCase(obj){
  const store = customCaseStore();
  store[obj.id] = obj;
  LS.set(KEYS.custom, store);
  CASES[obj.id] = obj;
  if (!archiveById(obj.id)) {
    CUSTOM_ARCHIVES.push({ id:obj.id, type:'case', caseId:obj.id, hidden:false, custom:true,
      title: obj.title || ('自定义案件 ' + obj.id), subtitle: obj.subtitle || '用户导入卷宗',
      dept:'案件工坊', year:'——', place:'未知', tags:['自定义'],
      brief: obj.brief || (obj.storyIntro || '').slice(0, 80), body: obj.archiveBody || obj.storyIntro || '' });
  }
}
function removeCustomCase(id){
  const store = customCaseStore();
  delete store[id];
  LS.set(KEYS.custom, store);
  delete CASES[id];
  for (let i = CUSTOM_ARCHIVES.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (CUSTOM_ARCHIVES[i].id === id) CUSTOM_ARCHIVES.splice(i, 1);
  for (let s = 1; s <= 3; s++) LS.del(KEYS.save(id, s));
  LS.del(KEYS.auto(id)); LS.del(KEYS.gallery(id));
}

/* ============================== 3. 路由与视图 ============================== */
const state = { route:'home', archiveFilter:'all', archiveQuery:'', detail:null };

function navDef(){ return [
  { k:'home',     n:'首页',     i:'🏠' },
  { k:'archives', n:'档案库',   i:'🗂' },
  { k:'roster',   n:'人物',     i:'👥' },
  { k:'notice',   n:'公告',     i:'📢' },
  { k:'about',    n:'关于',     i:'ℹ️' },
  { k:'progress', n:'总进度',   i:'📊' },
  { k:'profile',  n:'个人信息', i:'🪪' }
]; }

function go(route){
  state.route = route;
  state.detail = null;
  try { if (window.location.hash !== '#/' + route) window.location.hash = '#/' + route; } catch (e) {}
  render();
  window.scrollTo({ top:0, behavior:'smooth' });
}
function routeFromHash(){
  try {
    const h = (window.location.hash || '').replace(/^#\/?/, '').trim();
    if (h && navDef().some(n => n.k === h)) return h;
    if (h.indexOf('case/') === 0) return 'archives';
  } catch (e) {}
  return 'home';
}

function render(){
  const nav = $('#nav');
  if (!nav) return;
  nav.innerHTML = navDef().map(n =>
    '<button class="nav-btn ' + (state.route === n.k ? 'on' : '') + '" role="tab" aria-selected="' + (state.route === n.k) + '" data-route="' + n.k + '">' +
    '<span class="nav-ico" aria-hidden="true">' + n.i + '</span>' + n.n + '</button>').join('');
  nav.onclick = (ev) => { const b = ev.target.closest('[data-route]'); if (b) { SFX.play('click'); go(b.getAttribute('data-route')); } };

  const root = $('#view-root');
  const views = { home: homeView, archives: archiveView, progress: progressView, profile: profileView, roster: rosterView, notice: noticeView, about: aboutView };
  root.innerHTML = '<div class="view">' + (views[state.route] || homeView)() + '</div>';
  bindView();
  if (state.detail) renderDetail();
}

function renderDetail(){
  const old = $('#arc-detail'); if (old) old.remove();
  const d = state.detail; if (!d) return;
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'detail'; wrap.id = 'arc-detail';
  wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="detail-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="档案详情"></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(wrap);
  wrap.onclick = (ev) => {
    const act = ev.target.closest('[data-dact]');
    if (!act) { if (ev.target === wrap) closeDetail(); return; }
    const a = act.getAttribute('data-dact');
    if (a === 'close') closeDetail();
    else if (a === 'read') { state.detail = null; closeDetail(); }
    else if (a === 'play') { closeDetail(); startCaseFlow(d.arch.caseId); }
    else if (a === 'resume') { closeDetail(); startCaseFlow(d.arch.caseId, 'menu'); }
    else if (a === 'wipe') {
      confirmModal('清除该案全部进度', '将删除 ' + d.arch.caseId + ' 的 3 个存档槽、自动存档与结局图鉴。\n全局进度（结案次数、隐藏档案解锁）不受影响。', () => {
        for (let s = 1; s <= 3; s++) LS.del(KEYS.save(d.arch.caseId, s));
        LS.del(KEYS.auto(d.arch.caseId)); LS.del(KEYS.gallery(d.arch.caseId));
        toast('已清除 ' + d.arch.caseId + ' 的本地进度', 'green', '🧹');
        closeDetail(); render();
      }, '确认清除');
    }
    else if (a === 'custom-del') {
      confirmModal('移除导入卷宗', '将从案件工坊移除 ' + d.arch.id + '，并删除其存档。', () => { removeCustomCase(d.arch.id); closeDetail(); render(); toast('已移除导入卷宗', 'green', '🗑'); }, '移除');
    }
  };
  const panel = wrap.querySelector('.detail-panel');
  panel.innerHTML = detailHTML(d.arch);
  SFX.play('modal');
}
function closeDetail(){ const el = $('#arc-detail'); if (el) el.remove(); state.detail = null; }

function detailHTML(arch){
  const isCase = arch.type === 'case';
  const st = isCase ? caseStatus(arch.caseId) : 'sealed';
  const gal = isCase ? getGallery(arch.caseId) : {};
  const C = isCase ? CASES[arch.caseId] : null;
  const endingKeys = C && C.endings ? Object.keys(C.endings) : [];
  const rec = getGlobalProgress().records[arch.caseId] || null;
  let h = '';
  h += '<div class="detail-head"><span class="chip gold mono">' + esc(arch.id) + '</span>' +
       '<div style="flex:1"><h3>' + esc(arch.title) + '</h3>' +
       '<div style="color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12.5px">' + esc(arch.subtitle || '') + '</div></div>' +
       '<button class="x-btn" data-dact="close" aria-label="关闭">✕</button></div>';
  h += '<div class="archive-tags" style="margin:10px 0 0">' +
       (arch.tags || []).map(t => '<span class="chip">' + esc(t) + '</span>').join('') +
       (isCase ? '<span class="chip ' + STATUS[st].chip + '">' + STATUS[st].label + '</span>' : '<span class="chip gold">隐藏档案</span>') + '</div>';

  h += '<div class="detail-body">';
  h += '<dl class="kv">' +
       '<dt>科室</dt><dd>' + esc(arch.dept || '—') + '</dd>' +
       '<dt>年代</dt><dd>' + esc(arch.year || '—') + '</dd>' +
       '<dt>地点</dt><dd>' + esc(arch.place || '—') + '</dd>' +
       '<dt>状态</dt><dd>' + (isCase ? STATUS[st].label : (arch.unlock ? '已解锁 · ' + arch.unlock.label : '封存')) + '</dd>' +
       (rec ? '<dt>结案</dt><dd>' + rec.completed + ' 次 · 真结局 ' + (rec.trueEnded ? '已达成' : '未达成') + '</dd>' : '') +
       '</dl>';
  if (arch.brief) h += '<div><div class="sp-title" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em;color:var(--ink-4)">案情摘要</div>' +
       '<div class="doc-text">' + esc(arch.brief) + '</div></div>';
  if (arch.body) h += '<div><div class="sp-title" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em;color:var(--ink-4)">档案正文</div>' +
       '<div class="doc-text">' + esc(arch.body) + '</div></div>';

  if (isCase && endingKeys.length) {
    h += '<div><div class="sp-title" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em;color:var(--ink-4)">结局图鉴 ' + Object.keys(gal).length + '/' + endingKeys.length + '</div>';
    endingKeys.forEach(k => {
      const e = C.endings[k], got = !!gal[k];
      h += '<div class="gal-card ' + (got ? (e.true ? 'true' : 'got') : '') + '" style="margin-bottom:6px">' +
           '<span class="gi" aria-hidden="true">' + (got ? (e.true ? '★' : '◆') : '◇') + '</span>' +
           '<div style="flex:1"><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:560">' + (got ? esc(e.title) : '？？？') + '</div>' +
           '<div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-3)">' + (got ? esc(e.sub || '') : '尚未达成的结局') + '</div></div>' +
           '<span class="chip ' + (e.true ? 'gold' : '') + '">' + esc(e.kindLabel || (e.true ? '真结局' : '结局')) + '</span></div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
  }

  h += '<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:6px">';
  if (isCase) {
    h += '<button class="btn primary" data-dact="play">开始调查</button>';
    h += '<button class="btn" data-dact="resume">存档 / 读档</button>';
    if (st !== 'open') h += '<button class="btn danger" data-dact="wipe">清除进度</button>';
  } else {
    h += '<button class="btn" data-dact="read">已阅</button>';
  }
  if (arch.custom) h += '<button class="btn danger" data-dact="custom-del">移除卷宗</button>';
  h += '</div>';
  h += '</div>';
  return h;
}
/* ---------------------------- 首页 ---------------------------- */
function homeView(){
  const p = getProfile(), prog = getGlobalProgress();
  const cases = allArchives().filter(a => a.type === 'case');
  const secrets = allArchives().filter(a => a.hidden);
  const unlockedSecrets = secrets.filter(a => isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog));
  const cleared = cases.filter(a => { const r = prog.records[a.caseId]; return r && r.completed > 0; }).length;
  const trueCount = cases.filter(a => { const r = prog.records[a.caseId]; return r && r.trueEnded; }).length;
  const lastCaseId = getSettings().lastCase;
  const lastArch = lastCaseId ? archiveById(lastCaseId) : null;

  let h = '';
  /* ---- 大屏封面 ---- */
  h += '<div class="hero-big">' +
    '<div class="hero-brand" aria-hidden="true">BA</div>' +
    '<h1 class="hero-title-big">第九调查局</h1>' +
    '<div class="hero-subtitle">BUREAU ARCHIVE · 内部终端</div>' +
    '<div class="hero-tagline">' +
      '<span class="tq">每一份档案都需要一个读者。</span><br>' +
      '程序需要一个变量，故事需要一个位置。<br>' +
      '你已经读到的这些卷宗，还在等下一行批注。' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="hero-actions">' +
      (lastArch ? '<button class="btn primary" data-act="quick-continue">继续 ' + esc(lastArch.id) + '</button>' : '') +
      '<button class="btn' + (lastArch ? '' : ' primary') + '" data-act="quick-archives">进入档案库</button>' +
      '<button class="btn ghost" data-act="quick-about">关于调查局</button>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="hero-stats">' +
      '<span><b>' + cleared + '</b>/ ' + cases.length + ' 已结案</span>' +
      '<span><b>' + trueCount + '</b> 真结局</span>' +
      '<span><b>' + unlockedSecrets.length + '</b>/ ' + secrets.length + ' 隐藏档案</span>' +
      '<span>' + esc(greetWord()) + ' · ' + esc(p.name) + '</span>' +
    '</div>' +
  '</div>';

  /* ---- 统计卡 ---- */
  h += '<div class="stat-grid">' +
    statCard('已结案案件', cleared, '<small>/ ' + cases.length + '</small>', '累计结案 ' + Object.keys(prog.records).reduce((n, k) => n + (prog.records[k].completed || 0), 0) + ' 次') +
    statCard('真结局', trueCount, '<small>/ ' + cases.length + '</small>', trueCount >= cases.length ? '全部真相已归档' : '继续复核未竟之案') +
    statCard('隐藏档案', unlockedSecrets.length, '<small>/ ' + secrets.length + '</small>', unlockedSecrets.length ? '已解锁：' + unlockedSecrets.map(a => a.id).join('、') : '达成真结局可解锁') +
    statCard('累计游玩', Math.floor((prog.playMs || 0) / 60000), '<small>分钟</small>', '累计收集证据 ' + (prog.evidenceTotal || 0) + ' 件') +
  '</div>';

  /* ---- 快捷入口 ---- */
  h += '<div class="section-title">快捷入口 <span class="tag">QUICK ACCESS</span></div><div class="grid-3">';
  const quick = [
    { ico:'🕯️', t: lastArch ? ('继续 ' + lastArch.id) : '开始调查', d: lastArch ? lastArch.title : '从档案库选择一份卷宗', act:'quick-continue' },
    { ico:'🗂', t:'档案库', d: cases.length + ' 份在册卷宗 · ' + secrets.length + ' 份隐藏档案', act:'quick-archives' },
    { ico:'👥', t:'人物名录', d:'局内成员与关联人物', act:'quick-roster' },
    { ico:'📢', t:'公告栏', d:'局内通告与更新日志', act:'quick-notice' },
    { ico:'ℹ️', t:'关于调查局', d:'世界观 · 部门架构 · 归档规则', act:'quick-about' },
    { ico:'📊', t:'总进度', d:'跨案件统计与解锁进度', act:'quick-progress' },
    { ico:'🔧', t:'案件工坊', d:'导入 / 导出 JSON 卷宗', act:'quick-workshop' },
    { ico:'⚙️', t:'设置', d:'音效 · 自动存档 · 打字机', act:'quick-settings' }
  ];
  quick.forEach(q => {
    h += '<button class="quick" data-act="' + q.act + '"><span class="quick-ico" aria-hidden="true">' + q.ico + '</span>' +
         '<span><span class="quick-t">' + esc(q.t) + '</span><span class="quick-d" style="display:block">' + esc(q.d) + '</span></span></button>';
  });
  h += '</div>';

  /* ---- 最近档案 ---- */
  h += '<div class="section-title">最近档案 <span class="tag">RECENT</span></div><div class="grid-2">';
  const recents = cases.map(a => {
    const r = prog.records[a.caseId];
    let ts = r ? r.lastAt : 0;
    for (let s = 1; s <= 3; s++) { const sv = LS.get(KEYS.save(a.caseId, s), null); if (sv && sv.ts && sv.ts > ts) ts = sv.ts; }
    const av = LS.get(KEYS.auto(a.caseId), null); if (av && av.ts && av.ts > ts) ts = av.ts;
    return { a: a, ts: ts, r: r };
  }).filter(x => x.ts > 0).sort((x, y) => y.ts - x.ts).slice(0, 4);
  if (!recents.length) {
    h += '<div class="empty" style="grid-column:1/-1"><span class="big">🗄</span>暂无调查记录。前往档案库开启第一份卷宗。</div>';
  } else {
    recents.forEach(x => {
      const st = caseStatus(x.a.caseId);
      h += '<div class="card tight"><div class="archive-top"><div style="flex:1">' +
        '<div class="archive-id">' + esc(x.a.id) + '</div><div class="archive-title">' + esc(x.a.title) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="archive-sub">' + new Date(x.ts).toLocaleString('zh-CN') + '</div></div>' +
        '<span class="chip ' + STATUS[st].chip + '">' + STATUS[st].label + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn sm primary" data-act="open-case" data-case="' + esc(x.a.caseId) + '">进入</button>' +
        '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="open-detail" data-arch="' + esc(x.a.id) + '">档案</button></div></div>';
    });
  }
  h += '</div>';

  /* ---- 局内通告（精简版，完整公告去公告栏） ---- */
  h += '<div class="section-title">局内通告 <span class="tag">NOTICE</span><span style="flex:1"></span>' +
       '<a href="#/notice" data-act="quick-notice" style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--accent)">查看全部 →</a></div>' +
    '<div class="card">' +
    '<div class="notice-item"><div class="notice-date">内部 · 常设</div><div class="notice-body">' +
      '<div class="notice-title">存档与自动存档</div>' +
      '<div class="notice-text">每桩案件共 3 个手动存档槽，另有独立自动存档槽（每次场景切换写入），两者互不影响。</div>' +
    '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="notice-item"><div class="notice-date">内部 · 常设</div><div class="notice-body">' +
      '<div class="notice-title">结案报告卡</div>' +
      '<div class="notice-text">结案时生成报告卡：评级（S/A/C/D/F）、耗时、证据 / 关联 / 问询 / 成就完成度一览。</div>' +
    '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="notice-item"><div class="notice-date">内部 · 常设</div><div class="notice-body">' +
      '<div class="notice-title">隐藏档案</div>' +
      '<div class="notice-text">隐藏档案不出现在档案库中，需在对应案件达成真结局后自动解锁。</div>' +
    '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="notice-item"><div class="notice-date">键盘</div><div class="notice-body">' +
      '<div class="notice-title">快捷键</div>' +
      '<div class="notice-text"><b>1-9</b> 选择行动 · <b>Tab</b> 切换侧栏 · <b>S</b> 存档 · <b>C</b> 关联台 · <b>?</b> 键位表 · <b>Esc</b> 最小化</div>' +
    '</div></div>' +
    (isMobile() ? '<div class="notice-item"><div class="notice-date">移动端</div><div class="notice-body">' +
      '<div class="notice-title">添加到主屏幕</div>' +
      '<div class="notice-text">浏览器菜单里选「添加到主屏幕」，可全屏、无地址栏读档案；横竖屏均可。</div>' +
    '</div></div>' : '') +
    '</div>';
  return h;
}
function statCard(k, v, suffix, foot){
  return '<div class="stat"><div class="stat-k">' + esc(k) + '</div><div class="stat-v">' + v + (suffix || '') + '</div><div class="stat-f">' + foot + '</div></div>';
}
function notice(t){ return '<div class="tl-item"><span class="tl-dot"></span><span class="tl-txt">' + t + '</span></div>'; }

/* ---------------------------- 档案库 ---------------------------- */
function archiveView(){
  const prog = getGlobalProgress();
  const q = state.archiveQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = allArchives().filter(a => {
    if (a.hidden && !isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog)) return false;      /* 未解锁的隐藏档案：不可见 */
    if (state.archiveFilter === 'case' && a.type !== 'case') return false;
    if (state.archiveFilter === 'hidden' && !a.hidden) return false;
    if (state.archiveFilter === 'true') { if (a.type !== 'case' || caseStatus(a.caseId) !== 'true') return false; }
    if (state.archiveFilter === 'done') { const r = prog.records[a.caseId]; if (a.type !== 'case' || !(r && r.completed > 0)) return false; }
    if (q) {
      const hay = (a.id + ' ' + a.title + ' ' + (a.subtitle || '') + ' ' + (a.brief || '') + ' ' + (a.tags || []).join(' ')).toLowerCase();
      if (hay.indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  let h = '';
  h += '<div class="page-head"><div><h1 class="page-title">档案库</h1>' +
       '<p class="page-desc">在册卷宗 ' + allArchives().filter(a => a.type === 'case').length + ' 份 · 隐藏档案 ' +
       allArchives().filter(a => a.hidden && isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog)).length + ' 份已解锁</p></div></div>';

  h += '<div class="filters">' +
    '<div class="search-wrap"><span class="ico" aria-hidden="true">🔍</span>' +
    '<input id="arc-q" class="search" type="search" placeholder="搜索案件编号 / 标题 / 标签…" value="' + esc(state.archiveQuery) + '" aria-label="搜索档案"></div>' +
    '<div class="seg" id="arc-seg">' +
      segBtn('all', '全部') + segBtn('case', '在册案件') + segBtn('done', '已结案') + segBtn('true', '真结局') + segBtn('hidden', '隐藏档案') +
    '</div></div>';

  if (!list.length) {
    h += '<div class="empty"><span class="big">🔒</span>没有符合条件的档案。未解锁的隐藏档案不会显示在此处。</div>';
  } else {
    h += '<div class="archive-list">';
    list.forEach(a => { h += archiveCard(a, prog); });
    h += '</div>';
  }
  h += '<div class="section-title">档案库说明 <span class="tag">README</span></div><div class="card" style="color:var(--ink-3);font-size:13px">' +
    '· 点击卡片查看档案正文、结局图鉴与操作按钮。<br>' +
    '· 「清除进度」只删除该案的存档与图鉴，不影响全局结案次数与隐藏档案解锁。<br>' +
    '· 隐藏档案的解锁条件可在<a href="#/progress" data-act="quick-progress">总进度</a>页查看。' +
    (CUSTOM_ARCHIVES.length ? '<br>· 已从案件工坊导入 ' + CUSTOM_ARCHIVES.length + ' 份自定义卷宗。' : '') +
    '</div>';
  return h;
}
function segBtn(k, label){
  return '<button data-filter="' + k + '" class="' + (state.archiveFilter === k ? 'on' : '') + '">' + label + '</button>';
}
function archiveCard(a, prog){
  const isCase = a.type === 'case';
  const st = isCase ? caseStatus(a.caseId) : 'sealed';
  const gal = isCase ? getGallery(a.caseId) : {};
  const C = isCase ? CASES[a.caseId] : null;
  const endingKeys = C && C.endings ? Object.keys(C.endings) : [];
  const trueKey = endingKeys.filter(k => C.endings[k].true)[0];
  let h = '<article class="archive ' + (a.hidden ? 'secret' : '') + '" data-st="' + st + '">';
  h += '<div class="archive-top"><div style="flex:1;min-width:0">' +
       '<div class="archive-id">' + esc(a.id) + (a.custom ? ' · 工坊' : '') + '</div>' +
       '<div class="archive-title">' + esc(a.title) + '</div>' +
       '<div class="archive-sub">' + esc(a.subtitle || '') + '</div></div>' +
       '<span class="chip ' + STATUS[st].chip + '">' + STATUS[st].label + '</span></div>';
  h += '<div class="archive-sub" style="color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12.5px">' + esc((a.brief || '').slice(0, 92)) + ((a.brief || '').length > 92 ? '…' : '') + '</div>';
  h += '<div class="archive-tags">' + (a.tags || []).map(t => '<span class="chip">' + esc(t) + '</span>').join('') +
       '<span class="chip dim mono">' + esc(a.year || '——') + '</span></div>';
  h += '<div class="archive-foot">';
  if (endingKeys.length) {
    h += '<span class="gallery-dots" title="结局图鉴 ' + Object.keys(gal).length + '/' + endingKeys.length + '">' +
      endingKeys.map(k => '<i class="gdot ' + (gal[k] ? (C.endings[k].true ? 'true' : 'on') : '') + '"></i>').join('') + '</span>';
    if (trueKey && !gal[trueKey]) h += '<span class="chip dim">真结局未达成</span>';
  }
  h += '<span class="spacer"></span>';
  if (isCase) {
    h += '<button class="btn sm primary" data-act="open-case" data-case="' + esc(a.caseId) + '">' +
         (st === 'open' ? '开始调查' : '继续调查') + '</button>';
  }
  h += '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="open-detail" data-arch="' + esc(a.id) + '">档案</button>';
  h += '</div></article>';
  return h;
}

/* ---------------------------- 总进度 ---------------------------- */
function progressView(){
  const prog = getGlobalProgress();
  const cases = allArchives().filter(a => a.type === 'case');
  const secrets = allArchives().filter(a => a.hidden);
  const trueCount = cases.filter(a => { const r = prog.records[a.caseId]; return r && r.trueEnded; }).length;
  const totalClears = cases.reduce((n, a) => n + ((prog.records[a.caseId] && prog.records[a.caseId].completed) || 0), 0);
  const pct = cases.length ? trueCount / cases.length : 0;
  const R = 46, CIRC = 2 * Math.PI * R;
  const casesWithEndings = cases.reduce((n, a) => n + (CASES[a.caseId] && CASES[a.caseId].endings ? Object.keys(CASES[a.caseId].endings).length : 0), 0);
  const gotEndings = cases.reduce((n, a) => n + Object.keys(getGallery(a.caseId)).length, 0);

  let h = '';
  h += '<div class="page-head"><div><h1 class="page-title">总进度</h1>' +
       '<p class="page-desc">跨案件统计 · 隐藏档案解锁 · 归档总览</p></div></div>';

  h += '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:18px"><div class="ring-wrap">' +
    '<svg class="ring" viewBox="0 0 110 110" aria-hidden="true">' +
      '<circle class="bg" cx="55" cy="55" r="' + R + '"></circle>' +
      '<circle class="fg" cx="55" cy="55" r="' + R + '" stroke-dasharray="' + CIRC.toFixed(2) + '" stroke-dashoffset="' + (CIRC * (1 - pct)).toFixed(2) + '"></circle>' +
    '</svg>' +
    '<div style="flex:1;min-width:200px">' +
      '<div class="stat-k">真结局完成度</div>' +
      '<div class="stat-v">' + Math.round(pct * 100) + '<small>%</small></div>' +
      '<div class="stat-f">' + trueCount + ' / ' + cases.length + ' 个案件已侦破真相</div>' +
      '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(pct * 100) + '%"></i></div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div style="flex:1;min-width:200px">' +
      '<div class="stat-k">结局图鉴收集</div>' +
      '<div class="stat-v">' + gotEndings + '<small>/ ' + casesWithEndings + '</small></div>' +
      '<div class="stat-f">累计结案 ' + totalClears + ' 次 · 累计游玩 ' + fmtDuration(prog.playMs) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + (casesWithEndings ? Math.round(gotEndings / casesWithEndings * 100) : 0) + '%"></i></div>' +
    '</div>' +
  '</div></div>';

  h += '<div class="stat-grid" style="margin-bottom:6px">' +
    statCard('隐藏档案', secrets.filter(a => isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog)).length, '<small>/ ' + secrets.length + '</small>', '达成真结局自动解锁') +
    statCard('累计结案', totalClears, '<small>次</small>', '含重复结案') +
    statCard('累计游玩', Math.floor((prog.playMs || 0) / 60000), '<small>分钟</small>', fmtDuration(prog.playMs)) +
    statCard('累计证据', prog.evidenceTotal || 0, '<small>件</small>', '跨案件收集总数') +
  '</div>';

  h += '<div class="section-title">案件明细 <span class="tag">CASES</span></div>';
  h += '<div class="card tight" style="overflow-x:auto"><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px">' +
    '<thead><tr style="text-align:left;color:var(--ink-4);font-size:11.5px" class="mono">' +
    '<th style="padding:7px 8px">编号</th><th style="padding:7px 8px">案件</th><th style="padding:7px 8px">状态</th>' +
    '<th style="padding:7px 8px">结案</th><th style="padding:7px 8px">图鉴</th><th style="padding:7px 8px">操作</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  cases.forEach(a => {
    const r = prog.records[a.caseId] || null;
    const st = caseStatus(a.caseId);
    const C = CASES[a.caseId];
    const tot = C && C.endings ? Object.keys(C.endings).length : 0;
    const got = Object.keys(getGallery(a.caseId)).length;
    h += '<tr style="border-top:1px solid var(--line-soft)">' +
      '<td style="padding:9px 8px" class="mono">' + esc(a.id) + '</td>' +
      '<td style="padding:9px 8px">' + esc(a.title) + '</td>' +
      '<td style="padding:9px 8px"><span class="chip ' + STATUS[st].chip + '">' + STATUS[st].label + '</span></td>' +
      '<td style="padding:9px 8px" class="mono">' + (r ? r.completed + ' 次' : '—') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="padding:9px 8px" class="mono">' + got + '/' + tot + (r && r.trueEnded ? ' <span class="chip gold">真相</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="padding:9px 8px"><button class="btn sm" data-act="open-case" data-case="' + esc(a.caseId) + '">进入</button></td></tr>';
  });
  h += '</tbody></table></div>';

  h += '<div class="section-title">隐藏档案解锁面板 <span class="tag">CLASSIFIED</span></div>';
  h += '<div class="unlock-list">';
  secrets.forEach(a => {
    const got = isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog);
    h += '<div class="unlock ' + (got ? 'got' : 'locked') + '">' +
      '<span class="unlock-ico" aria-hidden="true">' + (got ? '📂' : '🔒') + '</span>' +
      '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0">' +
      '<div class="unlock-t">' + esc(a.title) + (got ? '<span class="chip gold">已解锁</span>' : '<span class="chip red">未解锁</span>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="unlock-d">' + esc(a.subtitle || '') + '</div>' +
      (got
        ? '<div class="req"><div class="req-row"><span class="req-box done">✓</span>解锁条件：' + esc(a.unlock ? a.unlock.label : '—') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="req-row"><span class="req-box done">✓</span>可在<a href="#/archives" data-act="quick-archives">档案库</a>中查阅全文</div></div>'
        : '<div class="req">' + unlockReqs(a, prog) + '</div>') +
      '</div></div>';
  });
  h += '</div>';
  h += '<div class="section-title">数据管理 <span class="tag">DATA</span></div><div class="card">' +
    '<div class="btn-row"><button class="btn" data-act="export-all">导出全部进度 (JSON)</button>' +
    '<button class="btn ghost" data-act="copy-report">复制归档报告</button>' +
    '<button class="btn danger" data-act="reset-global">清空全局进度</button></div>' +
    '<p style="color:var(--ink-4);font-size:12.5px;margin:12px 0 0">清空全局进度会重置结案次数、真结局记录与隐藏档案解锁状态；各案存档槽与图鉴不受影响。</p></div>';
  return h;
}
function unlockReqs(a, prog){
  const rows = [];
  const u = a.unlock || {};
  if (u.kind === 'true') {
    const rec = prog.records[u.caseId];
    const done = !!(rec && rec.trueEnded);
    rows.push(reqRow(done, '在 ' + u.caseId + ' 中达成真结局'));
    rows.push(reqRow(done, '当前：' + (rec && rec.completed ? '已结案 ' + rec.completed + ' 次' : '尚未结案')));
  } else if (u.kind === 'allTrue') {
    u.cases.forEach(c => {
      const rec = prog.records[c];
      rows.push(reqRow(!!(rec && rec.trueEnded), c + ' 达成真结局'));
    });
  } else {
    rows.push(reqRow(prog.unlocked.indexOf(a.id) >= 0, u.label || '特殊条件'));
  }
  return rows.join('');
}
function reqRow(done, label){ return '<div class="req-row"><span class="req-box ' + (done ? 'done' : '') + '">' + (done ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' + esc(label) + '</div>'; }

    toggleRow('sfx', '音效', '合成音提示（点击 / 拾取 / 成就）', s.sfx) +

    toggleRow('autosave', '自动存档', '每次场景切换写入独立自动存档槽', s.autosave) +

  h += '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="section-title" style="margin-top:0">案件工坊 <span class="tag">WORKSHOP</span></div>' +

    '<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:14px"><button class="btn ghost" data-act="export-all">导出全部进度</button>' +
    '<button class="btn ghost" data-act="import-progress">导入进度 JSON</button></div></div>';
  h += '</div></div>';
  return h;
}

    '· 标注 CLASSIFIED 的人物，其身份需在隐藏档案中查阅。<br>' +
    '· 名录会随新案件加入而扩充。</div>';
  return h;
}

/* ---------------------------- 关于调查局 ---------------------------- */
function aboutView(){
  let h = '';
  h += '<div class="page-head"><div><h1 class="page-title">关于调查局</h1>' +
       '<p class="page-desc">世界观 · 部门架构 · 归档规则 · 版本信息</p></div></div>';

  h += '<div class="about-block"><h3>世界观设定</h3>' +
    '<p>第九调查局是一个成立于 1951 年的内部机构，专职复核地方警署与民政部门报结的悬案卷宗。</p>' +
    '<p>它的工作方式只有一条：把每一份卷宗从头读到尾。因为档案会缺失页数，但顺序不会撒谎。</p>' +
    '<p>自成立以来，第九调查局在编人员始终未超过四人。每一份卷宗的复核人签字栏都留着一个编号：<span class="mono" style="color:var(--accent)">ARCHIVIST-07</span>。</p>' +
    '<p>该编号对应一份不存在的入职档案：入职日期早于调查局成立三十七年。</p></div>';

  h += '<div class="about-block"><h3>部门架构</h3>' +
    '<dl class="kv">' +
    '<dt>重案复核科</dt><dd>复核已结案的失踪、伤害类案件，重点核对物证与证词的时间线。</dd>' +
    '<dt>乡野事件科</dt><dd>处理村落、林区、渔港等非城市区域报告的异常事件。</dd>' +
    '<dt>特别事务科</dt><dd>处理空间异常、群体性知觉异常、身份混淆类案件。</dd>' +
    '<dt>海事调查科</dt><dd>配合港务局处理灯塔、渡轮、礁区相关案件。</dd>' +
    '<dt>铁路调查科</dt><dd>处理列车运行期间发生的乘客失踪、物品转移、站点异常事件。</dd>' +
    '<dt>档案室</dt><dd>保管全部在册与隐藏卷宗，并负责归档规则维护。</dd>' +
    '</dl></div>';

  h += '<div class="about-block"><h3>归档规则</h3>' +
    '<p><b>一、卷宗不销毁。</b>任何案件一旦立案，其全部材料（含撤销、误判、未结）永久保存。</p>' +
    '<p><b>二、隐藏卷宗不公开。</b>涉及在世人员隐私、未成年当事人、或调查局内部事务的卷宗，标记为隐藏档案，仅在对应案件达成真结局后解锁。</p>' +
    '<p><b>三、复核人签字不可代签。</b>每份卷宗末页的复核人签字栏，由实际完成复核的调查员本人签署。</p>' +
    '<p><b>四、批注不属于任何人的立场。</b>卷宗末页允许出现非官方笔迹的批注，但其内容不入正式结论。</p></div>';

  h += '<div class="about-block"><h3>版本与存储</h3>' +
    '<dl class="kv">' +
    '<dt>系统版本</dt><dd class="mono">BUREAU ARCHIVE v1.5</dd>' +
    '<dt>部署方式</dt><dd>单文件 HTML · 纯前端 · 可离线运行</dd>' +
    '<dt>数据存储</dt><dd class="mono">localStorage（本机浏览器）</dd>' +
    '<dt>存储键</dt><dd class="mono" style="font-size:11.5px">bureau-profile-v1 / bureau-global-progress-v1 / case_&lt;id&gt;_save_N / case_&lt;id&gt;_autosave / case_&lt;id&gt;_gallery</dd>' +
    '</dl>' +
    '<p style="margin-top:14px;color:var(--ink-4);font-size:12.5px">' +
    '全部进度保存在本机。换设备、清理浏览器数据或使用隐私模式会导致进度丢失。' +
    '建议在「个人信息 → 存储与键位」中定期使用「导出全部进度」备份为 JSON。</p></div>';

  h += '<div class="about-block"><h3>致读者</h3>' +
    '<p>这套系统里所有案件的灵感都来自一个共同的假设：<span class="tq" style="color:var(--accent)">异常不是超自然现象，而是有人用非常规的方式替另一个人活着。</span></p>' +
    '<p>钟楼里没有第四个人，但有一个被编制表除名的人，在夹层里守着一座钟；井底没有水鬼，但有一个十七岁的少年，用二十一年时间把"我还活着"敲成了传说；镜像公寓没有双重人格，但有一位姐姐用两年时间替妹妹撑住了一个孩子的人生。</p>' +
    '<p>每一桩案件的真相都不复杂——复杂的从来都是动机。</p>' +
    '<p style="color:var(--ink-4);font-style:italic;margin-top:14px">「把每一份档案读到最后一行。」<br>—— ARCHIVIST-07</p></div>';

  h += '<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:20px"><button class="btn primary" data-act="quick-archives">进入档案库</button>' +
       '<button class="btn" data-act="quick-notice">查看公告</button>' +
       '<button class="btn ghost" data-act="export-all">导出全部进度</button></div>';
  return h;
}
function field(id, label, val){ return '<label class="field"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><input type="text" id="' + id + '" value="' + esc(val) + '"></label>'; }
function toggleRow(k, t, d, on){
  return '<button class="quick" data-toggle="' + k + '" aria-pressed="' + !!on + '"><span class="quick-ico" aria-hidden="true">' + (on ? '🔊' : '🔇') + '</span>' +
    '<span style="flex:1"><span class="quick-t">' + esc(t) + '</span><span class="quick-d" style="display:block">' + esc(d) + '</span></span>' +
    '<span class="chip ' + (on ? 'green' : 'dim') + '">' + (on ? '开' : '关') + '</span></button>';
}
function keyRow(k, d){ return '<div class="key-row"><kbd>' + esc(k) + '</kbd><span>' + esc(d) + '</span></div>'; }

/* ---------------------------- 视图事件绑定 ---------------------------- */
function bindView(){
  const root = $('#view-root');
  if (state.route === 'archives') {
    const q = $('#arc-q');
    if (q) {
      q.oninput = () => { state.archiveQuery = q.value; const pos = q.selectionStart; render(); const nq = $('#arc-q'); if (nq) { nq.focus(); try { nq.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (e) {} } };
    }
    const seg = $('#arc-seg');
    if (seg) seg.onclick = (ev) => { const b = ev.target.closest('[data-filter]'); if (b) { state.archiveFilter = b.getAttribute('data-filter'); SFX.play('click'); render(); } };
  }
  if (state.route === 'profile') {
    const pick = $('#pf-avatar-pick');
    if (pick) pick.onclick = (ev) => {
      const b = ev.target.closest('[data-av]'); if (!b) return;
      const i = parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-av'), 10);
      saveProfile({ avatar: i }); SFX.play('click');
      $$('#pf-avatar-pick button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on'));
      b.classList.add('on');
      const img = $('#pf-avatar-img'); if (img) img.src = AVATARS[i % AVATARS.length];
    };
  }
  const acts = { 'save-profile': onSaveProfile, 'reset-profile': onResetProfile };
  root.onclick = (ev) => {
    const t = ev.target.closest('[data-act]');
    if (!t) return;
    const a = t.getAttribute('data-act');
    if (acts[a]) { acts[a](t); return; }
    if (a === 'open-detail') { SFX.play('click'); state.detail = { arch: archiveById(t.getAttribute('data-arch')) }; if (state.detail.arch) renderDetail(); return; }
    if (a === 'open-case') { SFX.play('click'); startCaseFlow(t.getAttribute('data-case')); return; }
    appAction(a, t);
  };
  $$('#view-root [data-toggle]').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => {
      const k = b.getAttribute('data-toggle');
      const cur = !!getSettings()[k];
      saveSettings({ [k]: !cur });
      toast((k === 'sfx' ? '音效' : k === 'typewriter' ? '打字机文本' : k === 'autosave' ? '自动存档' : '行动提示') + '已' + (!cur ? '开启' : '关闭'), 'green', cur ? '🔇' : '🔊');
      render();
    };
  });
}
function onSaveProfile(){
  saveProfile({
    name: ($('#pf-name').value || '').trim() || DEFAULT_PROFILE.name,
    codename: ($('#pf-codename').value || '').trim() || DEFAULT_PROFILE.codename,
    dept: ($('#pf-dept').value || '').trim() || DEFAULT_PROFILE.dept,
    rank: ($('#pf-rank').value || '').trim() || DEFAULT_PROFILE.rank,
    bio: ($('#pf-bio').value || '').trim(),
    signature: ($('#pf-sign').value || '').trim()
  });
  SFX.play('ok'); toast('个人信息已保存', 'green', '💾');
  render();
}
function onResetProfile(){
  confirmModal('恢复默认信息', '将把个人信息重置为默认值（不影响进度）。', () => {
    LS.set(KEYS.profile, DEFAULT_PROFILE); toast('已恢复默认', 'green', '↺'); render();
  }, '恢复默认');
}
function appAction(a, el){
  if (a === 'quick-continue') {
    const last = getSettings().lastCase;
    startCaseFlow(last || (CASE_ARCHIVES[0] && CASE_ARCHIVES[0].caseId));
} else if (a === 'quick-roster') { go('roster'); }
else if (a === 'quick-notice') { go('notice'); }
else if (a === 'quick-about') { go('about'); }
else if (a === 'quick-archives') { go('archives'); }
  else if (a === 'quick-progress') { go('progress'); }
  else if (a === 'quick-settings' || a === 'quick-keys') { go('profile'); toast(a === 'quick-keys' ? '键位表见「存储与键位」卡片' : '设置见「引擎设置」卡片', 'gold', a === 'quick-keys' ? '⌨️' : '⚙️'); }
  else if (a === 'quick-workshop') { go('profile'); setTimeout(() => { const t = $('#ws-json'); if (t) { t.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' }); t.focus(); } }, 120); }
  else if (a === 'ws-import') { workshopImport(); }
  else if (a === 'ws-template') { const t = $('#ws-json'); if (t) { t.value = JSON.stringify(workshopTemplate(), null, 2); toast('已填入示例模板，可直接导入', 'green', '🧩'); } }
  else if (a === 'ws-export-all') { workshopExport(); }
  else if (a === 'ws-clear') {
    confirmModal('清空导入卷宗', '将移除全部从案件工坊导入的卷宗及其存档。', () => {
      Object.keys(customCaseStore()).forEach(id => removeCustomCase(id));
      toast('已清空导入卷宗', 'green', '🗑'); render();
    }, '清空');
  }
  else if (a === 'export-all') { exportProgress(); }
  else if (a === 'import-progress') { importProgress(); }
  else if (a === 'copy-report') { copyReport(); }
  else if (a === 'reset-global') {
    confirmModal('清空全局进度', '结案次数、真结局记录、隐藏档案解锁状态将被重置。\n（各案存档与图鉴保留。）', () => {
      saveGlobalProgress(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_PROGRESS)));
      toast('全局进度已重置', 'green', '♻'); render();
    }, '确认清空');
  }
}
/* ============================== 4. 案件注册表 ============================== */
/* 数据契约（可用 JSON 完整表达，供案件工坊导入导出）：
   sceneData[id] = { name, desc, onEnter:[effect], actions:[action] }
   action = { id,label,type,target,item,doc,hint,icon,cost,san,money,once,cond,hideIf,effects,confirm,verdicts,fallback }
   type: goto | pick | doc | talk | press | combine | use | drop | flag | rest | verdict
   effect = { type:'flag'|'san'|'time'|'money'|'item'|'doc'|'toast'|'log'|'scene'|'ending'|'addAction'|'removeAction', ... }
   cond  = { flag|notFlag|item|noItem|conclusion|scene|visited|talked|combine|doc|money|timeGte|timeLte|sanGte|sanLte|
             evCountGte|foundGte|foundAll|ending|all:[..]|any:[..]|not:{..} }  或函数 st => boolean     */
