      text:'你在林区的雪地里迷路了。\n\n树一排一排地往后走，雪一层一层地压上来。手电筒没电了，指南针指针在盘面上打转。你听见了列车的声音——很轻，很远——但那声音在雪里飘，一会儿在左边，一会儿在右边。\n\n你把手撑在一棵松树上。树皮很粗糙，粗糙得像一件旧棉袄。\n\n搜救队找到你的时候，你的手里攥着一根松针。\n\n结案类型：调查中断。复核资格冻结。',
      epilogue:'后来接手的人翻开这份卷宗，在末页看到一行不属于任何人的批注：\n"他听见了列车声。那是他自己脑子里的声音——这个季节，岭北站没有车。"'
    },
    forced:{
      title:'终点站', sub:'时间到 · 强制结案', kind:'forced', kindLabel:'强制结案',
      text:'晚上六点，终点站的大钟响了。雪还在下，站台上的冰锥又长了一截。\n\n你手里还有三件物证没有关联，岭北站的脚印已经被新雪盖住。\n\n结案类型：强制结案。失踪人员一名，未寻获。',
      epilogue:'回乘务员室的路上，你看见 9 号车厢最后一排的灯还亮着。\n9-14 号座位上没有人。座位上的军大衣和铝饭盒已经被物证科带走了。\n\n只剩下信。\n\n工作人员说，那沓信最后一封的收件人栏还空着。他没有写。'
    }
  },
  summaryEpilogue:'他在那条线上一来一回八年。别人看他是来扫墓，其实他是来上班——守着一间没人知道的屋子，等一个不会回来的人。'
};
/* ============================== 5. 游戏引擎（共用） ============================== */
const PER_ACTION = 5;          /* 每个动作消耗的游戏内分钟 */
const REST_COST  = 10;         /* 休息动作默认经费消耗 */
const GRADE_MAP = { true:'S', good:'A', cold:'C', forced:'D', death:'F', fail:'F' };
const ENDING_KIND_LABEL = { true:'真结局', good:'良好结局', cold:'悬案', forced:'强制结案', death:'崩溃结局', fail:'失败结局' };

let CAS = null;                /* 当前案件（CAS 指针） */
let currentCaseId = '';
let st = null;                 /* 当前游戏状态 */
let UI = { tab:'files', sideCollapsed:false, showEnding:false, gameOpen:false, combineSlots:null, combineMsg:'', combineMsgOk:false };
let twTimer = null, lastTick = 0;

function curCase(){ return CAS || CASES[currentCaseId] || null; }

/* ---------------- 状态初始化 ---------------- */
function initGameState(C){
  return {
    v: 1,
    caseId: C.id,
    loc: C.initialLocation,
    time: C.initialTime || 0,
    san: C.initialSan == null ? 100 : C.initialSan,
    money: C.initialMoney || 0,
    flags: {},
    evidence: [],        /* 当前持有物证 */
    found: [],           /* 历史上找到过的物证（不因丢弃而减少） */
    conclusions: [],     /* 线索关联产生的结论 */
    docs: [],            /* 已阅文档 key */
    visited: {},         /* 进入过的场景 */
    sceneVisits: {},     /* 场景进入次数 */
    talked: [],          /* 已问询的对话 id（npcId.dlgId） */
    connected: {},       /* 已完成的配方 id */
    usedActions: {},     /* 已使用的一次性动作 id */
    achievements: {},    /* 已解锁成就 id */
    dynActions: {},      /* sceneId -> [action] 动态添加的行动 */
    log: [],             /* 调查日志 */
    ending: null,
    ended: false,
    startedAt: Date.now(),
    playMs: 0,
    stats: { actions:0, combines:0, failedCombines:0, dialogs:0, reads:0 }
  };
}
function clockText(minutes){
  const total = 9 * 60 + (minutes || 0);
  const h = Math.floor(total / 60), m = total % 60;
  return pad2(h % 24) + ':' + pad2(m);
}
function timeText(minutes){
  const m = minutes || 0;
  if (m < 60) return m + ' 分钟';
  return Math.floor(m / 60) + ' 时 ' + (m % 60) + ' 分';
}
function foundCount(s){ return (s || st).found.length; }
function ingredientOwned(name){
  return st.evidence.indexOf(name) >= 0 || st.conclusions.indexOf(name) >= 0;
}

/* ---------------- 声明式条件求值 ---------------- */
function condMet(c, s){
  s = s || st;
  if (c == null) return true;
  if (typeof c === 'function') { try { return !!c(s); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  if (Array.isArray(c)) return c.every(x => condMet(x, s));
  if (c.all) return c.all.every(x => condMet(x, s));
  if (c.any) return c.any.some(x => condMet(x, s));
  if (c.not) return !condMet(c.not, s);
  let ok = true;
  if ('flag' in c) ok = ok && (!!s.flags[c.flag] === ('value' in c ? !!c.value : true));
  if ('notFlag' in c) ok = ok && !s.flags[c.notFlag];
  if ('item' in c) ok = ok && s.evidence.indexOf(c.item) >= 0;
  if ('noItem' in c) ok = ok && s.evidence.indexOf(c.noItem) < 0;
  if ('conclusion' in c) ok = ok && s.conclusions.indexOf(c.conclusion) >= 0;
  if ('doc' in c) ok = ok && s.docs.indexOf(c.doc) >= 0;
  if ('scene' in c) ok = ok && s.loc === c.scene;
  if ('visited' in c) ok = ok && !!s.visited[c.visited];
  if ('talked' in c) ok = ok && s.talked.indexOf(c.talked) >= 0;
  if ('combine' in c) ok = ok && !!s.connected[c.combine];
  if ('ending' in c) ok = ok && !!(s.ending && s.ending.id === c.ending);
  if ('moneyGte' in c) ok = ok && s.money >= c.moneyGte;
  if ('sanGte' in c) ok = ok && s.san >= c.sanGte;
  if ('sanLte' in c) ok = ok && s.san <= c.sanLte;
  if ('timeGte' in c) ok = ok && s.time >= c.timeGte;
  if ('timeLte' in c) ok = ok && s.time <= c.timeLte;
  if ('evCountGte' in c) ok = ok && s.evidence.length >= c.evCountGte;
  if ('foundGte' in c) ok = ok && foundCount(s) >= c.foundGte;
  if ('foundAll' in c) ok = ok && foundCount(s) >= ((curCase() || {}).evidenceList || []).length;
  if ('combineCountGte' in c) ok = ok && Object.keys(s.connected).length >= c.combineCountGte;
  if ('achCountGte' in c) ok = ok && Object.keys(s.achievements).length >= c.achCountGte;
  return ok;
}
/* 自定义案件兼容：check 函数优先 */
function checkDef(def){
  if (!def) return false;
  if (typeof def.check === 'function') { try { return !!def.check(st); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  if (def.when !== undefined) return condMet(def.when, st);
  return false;
}

/* ---------------- 效果应用 ---------------- */
function addItem(name){
  if (!name) return;
  if (st.evidence.indexOf(name) < 0) st.evidence.push(name);
  if (st.found.indexOf(name) < 0) st.found.push(name);
  SFX.play('pick');
}
function removeItem(name){ const i = st.evidence.indexOf(name); if (i >= 0) st.evidence.splice(i, 1); }
function addConclusion(name){
  if (!name) return;
  if (st.conclusions.indexOf(name) < 0) st.conclusions.push(name);
  /* 注意：结论不计入"现场物证收集率"（found 只统计 evidenceList 中的物证） */
}
function changeSan(delta){
  if (!delta) return;
  st.san = clamp(st.san + delta, 0, 100);
  if (delta <= -6) SFX.play('scare'); else if (delta > 0) SFX.play('ok');
}
function advance(min){
  st.time += (min == null ? PER_ACTION : min);
}
function addLog(text){
  if (!text) return;
  st.log.push({ t: clockText(st.time), text: text });
  if (st.log.length > 200) st.log.shift();
}
function readDoc(key, quiet){
  if (!key) return;
  const C = curCase();
  const d = C && C.docTexts ? C.docTexts[key] : null;
  if (!d) { toast('文档缺失：' + key, 'red', '⚠'); return; }
  const first = st.docs.indexOf(key) < 0;
  if (first) { st.docs.push(key); st.stats.reads++; }
  SFX.play('page');
  if (!quiet || first) openDoc(key);
}
function applyEffects(list){
  if (!list) return;
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) list = [list];
  list.forEach(e => {
    if (!e || typeof e !== 'object') return;
    switch (e.type) {
      case 'flag': st.flags[e.key] = (e.value === undefined ? true : e.value); break;
      case 'san': changeSan(e.value || 0); break;
      case 'time': advance(e.value || 0); break;
      case 'money': st.money = Math.max(0, st.money + (e.value || 0)); break;
      case 'item': if (e.mode === 'remove') removeItem(e.item); else addItem(e.item); break;
      case 'conclusion': addConclusion(e.value || e.result); break;
      case 'doc': readDoc(e.key, false); break;
      case 'toast': toastInGame(e.text, e.kind, e.icon); break;
      case 'log': addLog(e.text); break;
      case 'scene': goScene(e.target); break;
      case 'ending': gameOver(e.id); break;
      case 'addAction': addDynamicAction(e.scene || st.loc, e.action); break;
      case 'removeAction': removeDynamicAction(e.scene || st.loc, e.id); break;
      default: break;
    }
  });
}
function addDynamicAction(sceneId, action){
  if (!sceneId || !action || !action.id) return;
  if (!st.dynActions[sceneId]) st.dynActions[sceneId] = [];
  const list = st.dynActions[sceneId];
  if (list.some(a => a.id === action.id)) return;          /* 去重 */
  list.push(action);
}
function removeDynamicAction(sceneId, id){
  const list = st.dynActions[sceneId];
  if (!list) return;
  for (let i = list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (list[i].id === id) list.splice(i, 1);
}

/* ---------------- 场景与行动 ---------------- */
function sceneDef(id){
  const C = curCase();
  return C && C.sceneData ? C.sceneData[id] : null;
}
function sceneActions(id){
  const sc = sceneDef(id);
  const base = sc && sc.actions ? sc.actions.slice() : [];
  const dyn = (st.dynActions[id] || []).slice();
  return base.concat(dyn);
}
function goScene(id, opts){
  const sc = sceneDef(id);
  if (!sc) { toast('场景不存在：' + id, 'red', '⚠'); return; }
  st.loc = id;
  st.sceneVisits[id] = (st.sceneVisits[id] || 0) + 1;
  const first = !st.visited[id];
  st.visited[id] = true;
  UI.showEnding = false;
  if (sc.onEnter) {
    sc.onEnter.forEach(e => {
      if (e && e.once && !first) return;
      applyEffects([e]);
    });
  }
  if (opts && opts.silent) return;
  checkAchievements();
  checkTasks();
  renderGame();
  if (getSettings().autosave) autosave();
}
function actionLabel(a){ return a.label || a.name || a.id; }
function actionIcon(a){
  if (a.icon) return a.icon;
  const map = { goto:'🚶', pick:'🤚', doc:'📄', talk:'💬', press:'❗', combine:'🧩', use:'🔧', drop:'🗑️', rest:'☕', verdict:'📮', flag:'👁️' };
  return map[a.type] || '·';
}
function isActionDone(a){
  if (st.usedActions[a.id]) return true;
  if (a.type === 'pick' && a.item && st.evidence.indexOf(a.item) >= 0) return true;
  return false;
}
function actionAvailable(a){
  if (a.hideIf && condMet(a.hideIf)) return false;
  if (a.once && isActionDone(a)) return false;
  return true;
}
function actionEnabled(a){
  if (a.cond && !condMet(a.cond)) return false;
  if (a.type === 'rest'){
    const cost = (a.money == null ? REST_COST : a.money);
    if (st.money < cost) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function costText(a){
  const t = (a.cost == null ? PER_ACTION : a.cost);
  const parts = [];
  if (t) parts.push(t + ' 分钟');
  if (a.type === 'rest') parts.push('¥' + (a.money == null ? REST_COST : a.money));
  else if (a.money) parts.push('¥' + a.money);
  if (a.san) parts.push((a.san > 0 ? '+' : '') + a.san + ' 专注');
  return parts.join(' · ');
}

/* 执行一个场景行动 */
function doAction(actionId){
  if (!st || st.ended) return;
  const a = sceneActions(st.loc).filter(x => x.id === actionId)[0];
  if (!a) return;
  if (!actionEnabled(a)) { SFX.play('bad'); toastInGame('条件未满足：' + (a.hint || '尚有前置线索'), '', '🔒'); return; }
  if (a.confirm) {
    if (HEADLESS) { runAction(a); return; }        /* 无头测试：跳过确认弹窗 */
    gModal({
      title:'确认行动',
      body:'<p style="margin:0;color:var(--ink-2);white-space:pre-wrap">' + esc(a.confirm) + '</p>',
      foot:'<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">再想想</button><button class="btn primary" data-g="go">确认</button>',
      onAction:(k) => { if (k === 'go') { closeGModal(); runAction(a); } }
    });
    return;
  }
  runAction(a);
}
function runAction(a){
  if (!st || st.ended) return;
  st.stats.actions++;
  st.usedActions[a.id] = true;
  advance(a.cost == null ? PER_ACTION : a.cost);
  if (a.money) st.money = Math.max(0, st.money - Math.abs(a.money));
  if (a.type === 'rest') st.money = Math.max(0, st.money - (a.money == null ? REST_COST : Math.abs(a.money)));
  if (a.san) changeSan(a.san);
  if (a.effects) applyEffects(a.effects);

  switch (a.type) {
    case 'goto': goScene(a.target); break;
    case 'pick':
      if (a.item) addItem(a.item);
      toastInGame('取得物证：' + a.item, 'gold', '📦');
      addLog('取得物证：' + a.item);
      if (a.doc) readDoc(a.doc, false);
      break;
    case 'doc': readDoc(a.doc || a.target, false); break;
    case 'talk':
    case 'press': openNpc(a.target, a.type === 'press'); break;
    case 'combine': openCombine(a.preset || null); break;
    case 'use':
      if (a.item) toastInGame('使用了 ' + a.item, 'ok', '🔧');
      break;
    case 'drop':
      if (a.item) { removeItem(a.item); toastInGame('丢弃了 ' + a.item, '', '🗑️'); }
      break;
    case 'rest':
      toastInGame('专注恢复了一些', 'green', '☕');
      addLog('休息片刻，专注恢复。');
      break;
    case 'verdict': evaluateVerdict(a); break;
    default: if (a.target && a.type === 'flag' && sceneDef(a.target)) goScene(a.target); break;
  }
  checkAchievements();
  checkTasks();
  if (!st.ended && st.time >= CAS.timeLimit) { gameOver('forced'); return; }
  if (!st.ended && st.san <= 0) { gameOver('collapse'); return; }
  renderGame();
  if (getSettings().autosave) autosave();
}

/* ---------------- 成就 / 任务 ---------------- */
function checkAchievements(){
  const C = curCase();
  if (!C || !C.achievements) return;
  Object.keys(C.achievements).forEach(id => {
    if (st.achievements[id]) return;
    const def = C.achievements[id];
    if (checkDef(def)) {
      st.achievements[id] = Date.now();
      achievementPop(def);
      addLog('成就解锁：' + def.name);
    }
  });
}
function allDialogues(){
  const C = curCase(); const out = [];
  if (!C || !C.npcData) return out;
  Object.keys(C.npcData).forEach(nid => {
    (C.npcData[nid].dialogues || []).forEach(d => out.push({ npc:nid, d:d }));
  });
  return out;
}
function checkTasks(){
  const C = curCase();
  if (!C) return [];
  const newly = [];
  [].concat(C.mainTasks || [], C.sideTasks || []).forEach(t => {
    if (!t) return;
    const done = checkDef(t);
    const was = st.flags['__task_' + t.id];
    st.flags['__task_' + t.id] = done;
    if (done && !was) newly.push(t);
  });
  newly.forEach(t => {
    toastInGame('任务完成：' + t.name, 'gold', '🎯');
    addLog('任务完成：' + t.name);
  });
  return newly;
}
function taskProgress(t){
  if (!t) return null;
  if (t.progressWhen && t.progressWhen.length) {
    const n = t.progressWhen.filter(c => condMet(c, st)).length;
    return [n, t.progressWhen.length];
  }
  if (t.progress && t.progress.foundOf) return [foundCount(st), t.progress.foundOf];
  if (t.progress && t.progress.of && t.progress.when) return [t.progress.when.filter(c => condMet(c, st)).length, t.progress.of];
  return null;
}

/* ---------------- 问询 ---------------- */
function npcDef(id){ const C = curCase(); return C && C.npcData ? C.npcData[id] : null; }
function openNpc(id, pressMode){
  const npc = npcDef(id);
  if (!npc) { toast('未找到该人物', 'red', '⚠'); return; }
  const list = (npc.dialogues || []).map(d => {
    const key = id + '.' + d.id;
    const used = st.talked.indexOf(key) >= 0;
    const locked = d.cond ? !condMet(d.cond) : false;
    return { d:d, key:key, used:used, locked:locked };
  });
  let body = '<div class="npc-card"><span class="npc-av" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(npc.avatar || '👤') + '</span>' +
    '<div><div class="npc-n">' + esc(npc.name) + '</div><div class="npc-d">' + esc(npc.desc || '') + '</div></div></div>';
  body += '<div class="sp-title">' + (pressMode ? '追问（需要前置线索）' : '问询 / 追问') + '</div><div class="opt" id="g-npc-opts">';
  const avail = list.filter(x => !x.locked && !(x.d.once && x.used));
  if (!avail.length) body += '<div class="no-ev">没有可以再问的了。</div>';
  list.forEach(x => {
    const hidden = x.d.once && x.used;
    if (hidden) return;
    body += '<button class="opt-btn ' + (x.d.press ? 'press' : '') + ' ' + (x.locked ? 'lock' : '') + '" ' +
      (x.locked ? 'disabled' : '') + ' data-dlg="' + esc(x.d.id) + '">' +
      '<span class="oi" aria-hidden="true">' + (x.locked ? '🔒' : x.d.press ? '❗' : '💬') + '</span>' +
      '<span style="flex:1">' + esc(x.d.label) + (x.d.once ? '' : '') + '</span>' +
      (x.d.cost ? '<span class="chip dim mono">' + x.d.cost + 'm</span>' : '') + '</button>';
  });
  body += '</div><div id="g-npc-text" style="margin-top:13px"></div>';
  gModal({
    title:'问询 · ' + npc.name,
    body:body,
    foot:'<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">结束交谈</button>',
    onAction:(k) => { if (k === 'close') closeGModal(); },
    onMount:(box) => {
      const opts = box.querySelector('#g-npc-opts');
      if (opts) opts.onclick = (ev) => {
        const b = ev.target.closest('[data-dlg]');
        if (!b || b.disabled) return;
        const d = (npc.dialogues || []).filter(x => x.id === b.getAttribute('data-dlg'))[0];
        if (d) askDialogue(id, d, box);
      };
    }
  });
}
function askDialogue(npcId, d, box){
  const key = npcId + '.' + d.id;
  const first = st.talked.indexOf(key) < 0;
  if (first) st.talked.push(key);
  st.stats.dialogs++;
  advance(d.cost == null ? PER_ACTION : d.cost);
  if (d.san) changeSan(d.san);
  if (d.money) st.money = Math.max(0, st.money - Math.abs(d.money));
  SFX.play('click');
  const holder = box ? box.querySelector('#g-npc-text') : null;
  if (holder) {
    holder.innerHTML = '<div class="dlg ' + (d.press ? 'press' : '') + '">' + esc(d.text || '') + '</div>';
    holder.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'nearest' });
  }
  if (d.effects) applyEffects(d.effects);
  if (d.item) addItem(d.item);
  if (d.doc) readDoc(d.doc, false);
  addLog('问询 ' + (npcDef(npcId) || {}).name + '：' + d.label);
  checkAchievements();
  checkTasks();
  if (box) {
    const btns = box.querySelectorAll('[data-dlg]');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(btns, b => {
      if (b.getAttribute('data-dlg') === d.id) {
        if (d.once) { b.style.display = 'none'; }
        else { b.classList.add('used'); }
      }
    });
  }
  if (!st.ended && st.time >= CAS.timeLimit) { closeGModal(); gameOver('forced'); return; }
  if (!st.ended && st.san <= 0) { closeGModal(); gameOver('collapse'); return; }
  renderGame();
  if (getSettings().autosave) autosave();
}

/* ---------------- 合成（线索关联） ---------------- */
function openCombine(preset){
  const C = curCase();
  const pool = st.evidence.concat(st.conclusions);
  if (pool.length < 2) { toastInGame('至少需要两件物证才能建立关联', '', '🧩'); return; }
  if (!preset) UI.combineMsg = '';
  const rec = UI.combineSlots || (UI.combineSlots = [null, null]);
  if (preset) rec[0] = preset;
  const render = () => {
    let body = '<div class="combine-box">' +
      '<div class="slots">' +
        slotHTML(0, rec[0]) + '<span class="slot-op" aria-hidden="true">＋</span>' + slotHTML(1, rec[1]) +
      '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-4);text-align:center;margin-bottom:11px">从下方选择两件物证放入合成槽，系统会尝试建立关联。</div>' +
      '<div class="sp-title">可用物证 / 结论（' + pool.length + '）</div><div class="pick-list" id="g-pick">';
    pool.forEach((name, i) => {
      const used = rec.indexOf(name) >= 0;
      body += '<button class="pick-item ' + (used ? 'on' : '') + '" data-pick="' + esc(name) + '">' +
        '<span aria-hidden="true">' + esc(itemIcon(name)) + '</span><span style="flex:1">' + esc(name) + '</span>' +
        (st.conclusions.indexOf(name) >= 0 ? '<span class="chip cyan">结论</span>' : '') + '</button>';
    });
    body += '</div></div><div id="g-combine-msg" style="margin-top:12px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-3)">' +
      (UI.combineMsg ? '<span style="color:' + (UI.combineMsgOk ? '#8fdcae' : '#ef9b9a') + '">' + esc(UI.combineMsg) + '</span>' : '') +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">已知关联（' + Object.keys(st.connected).length + '/' + ((C.recipes || []).length) + '）</div>';
    (C.recipes || []).forEach(r => {
      const got = st.connected[r.id];
      body += '<div class="task-card ' + (got ? 'done' : '') + '" style="padding:9px 11px">' +
        '<div class="task-h"><span class="tk">' + esc(r.id) + '</span>' + (got ? esc(r.result) : '？？？') + '</div>' +
        (got ? '<div class="task-c">' + esc(r.desc) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    });
    return body;
  };
  const mount = (box) => {
    const pick = box.querySelector('#g-pick');
    if (pick) pick.onclick = (ev) => {
      const b = ev.target.closest('[data-pick]');
      if (!b) return;
      const name = b.getAttribute('data-pick');
      const at = rec.indexOf(name);
      if (at >= 0) rec[at] = null;
      else if (!rec[0]) rec[0] = name;
      else if (!rec[1]) rec[1] = name;
      else rec[1] = name;
      SFX.play('click');
      refreshGModal(render(), mount);
    };
  };
  gModal({
    title:'线索关联台',
    wide:true,
    body:render(),
    foot:'<button class="btn ghost" data-g="clear">清空</button>' +
         '<button class="btn primary" data-g="try">建立关联</button>' +
         '<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">关闭</button>',
    onAction:(k, box) => {
      if (k === 'close') { UI.combineSlots = [null, null]; closeGModal(); return; }
      if (k === 'clear') { rec[0] = rec[1] = null; refreshGModal(render(), mount); return; }
      if (k === 'try') { tryCombine(rec[0], rec[1]); if (!st.ended) refreshGModal(render(), mount); }
    },
    onMount:mount
  });
}
function slotHTML(i, val){
  return '<button class="slot ' + (val ? 'filled' : '') + '" data-slot="' + i + '">' +
    (val ? '<span class="sl-n">' + esc(itemIcon(val)) + ' ' + esc(val) + '</span><span class="sl-x">点击移除</span>'
         : '<span class="sl-n">空槽 ' + (i + 1) + '</span><span class="sl-x">从下方选择</span>') + '</button>';
}
function itemIcon(name){
  const C = curCase();
  if (C && C.itemIcons && C.itemIcons[name]) return C.itemIcons[name];
  return '🔖';
}
function tryCombine(a, b){
  if (!a || !b) { toastInGame('请先放入两件物证', '', '🧩'); return false; }
  const C = curCase();
  const rec = (C.recipes || []).filter(r =>
    (r.a === a && r.b === b) || (r.a === b && r.b === a))[0];
  if (!rec) {
    st.stats.failedCombines++;
    SFX.play('bad');
    UI.combineMsg = '这两件物证之间没有可建立的关联。换一件再试。';
    UI.combineMsgOk = false;
    toastInGame('这两件东西之间建立不起关联', '', '✖');
    return false;
  }
  if (st.connected[rec.id]) {
    UI.combineMsg = '这个关联已经建立过了。';
    UI.combineMsgOk = false;
    toastInGame('这个关联已经建立过了', '', 'ℹ');
    return false;
  }
  st.connected[rec.id] = Date.now();
  st.stats.combines++;
  advance(PER_ACTION);
  addConclusion(rec.result);
  SFX.play('unlock');
  UI.combineMsg = '关联成立：' + rec.result + '　—— ' + rec.desc;
  UI.combineMsgOk = true;
  if (rec.effect) applyEffects(rec.effect);
  toastInGame('关联成立：' + rec.result, 'gold', '🔗', 4200);
  addLog('线索关联：' + rec.result);
  UI.combineSlots = [null, null];
  checkAchievements();
  checkTasks();
  renderGame();
  if (!st.ended && st.time >= CAS.timeLimit) { closeGModal(); gameOver('forced'); return true; }
  if (!st.ended && st.san <= 0) { closeGModal(); gameOver('collapse'); return true; }
  if (getSettings().autosave) autosave();
  return true;
}
/* 证据页里的"使用 / 丢弃" */
function useEvidence(name){
  const C = curCase();
  const acts = sceneActions(st.loc).filter(a => (a.type === 'use' || a.type === 'combine') && a.item === name);
  if (acts.length) { runAction(acts[0]); return; }
  const desc = (C.itemDesc && C.itemDesc[name]) || '一件安静的物证。';
  gModal({
    title:name,
    body:'<div style="display:flex;gap:13px;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:14px">' +
      '<span style="width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:12px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:22px;background:var(--panel-3);border:1px solid var(--line)" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(itemIcon(name)) + '</span>' +
      '<div style="flex:1;color:var(--ink-2);white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13.6px">' + esc(desc) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="sp-title">可建立的关联提示</div>' +
      ((C.recipes || []).filter(r => r.a === name || r.b === name)
        .map(r => '<div class="task-card" style="padding:9px 11px"><div class="task-h">' + esc(r.a) + ' ＋ ' + esc(r.b) + '</div>' +
          (st.connected[r.id] ? '<div class="task-c">' + esc(r.result) + '</div>' : '<div class="task-c" style="color:var(--ink-4)">尚未建立</div>') + '</div>').join('') ||
        '<div class="no-ev">这件物证暂时没有可组合的对象。</div>'),
    foot:'<button class="btn ghost" data-g="drop">丢弃</button>' +
         '<button class="btn primary" data-g="combine">放入关联台</button>' +
         '<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">关闭</button>',
    onAction:(k) => {
      if (k === 'close') closeGModal();
      else if (k === 'drop') {
        confirmGModal('丢弃物证', '确定丢弃「' + name + '」？丢弃后仍会计入收集率，但将无法用于关联。', () => {
          removeItem(name); toastInGame('已丢弃：' + name, '', '🗑️'); closeGModal(); renderGame();
        });
      } else if (k === 'combine') { closeGModal(); openCombine(name); }
    }
  });
}

/* ---------------- 结局判定 ---------------- */
function evaluateVerdict(a){
  const C = curCase();
  let pickId = null;
  (a.verdicts || []).forEach(v => { if (!pickId && condMet(v.cond)) pickId = v.ending; });
  if (!pickId) pickId = a.fallback || 'cold';
  if (!C.endings || !C.endings[pickId]) {
    const keys = C.endings ? Object.keys(C.endings) : [];
    pickId = keys.length ? keys[0] : null;
  }
  if (!pickId) { toast('该案件未定义结局', 'red', '⚠'); return; }
  gameOver(pickId);
}
function endingMeta(id){
  const C = curCase();
  const e = (C.endings && C.endings[id]) || {};
  const kind = e.kind || (e.true ? 'true' : 'cold');
  return {
    id: id,
    title: e.title || '结局',
    sub: e.sub || '',
    text: e.text || '（本案未提供结局文本。）',
    epilogue: e.epilogue || (typeof C.summaryEpilogue === 'string' ? C.summaryEpilogue : ''),
    kind: kind,
    kindLabel: e.kindLabel || ENDING_KIND_LABEL[kind] || '结局',
    grade: e.grade || GRADE_MAP[kind] || 'C',
    isTrue: kind === 'true'
  };
}
function buildReport(meta){
  const C = curCase();
  const totalEv = (C.evidenceList || []).length;
  const totalRec = (C.recipes || []).length;
  const dlgAll = allDialogues();
  const totalAch = C.achievements ? Object.keys(C.achievements).length : 0;
  return {
    caseId: C.id,
    caseTitle: C.title,
    kindLabel: meta.kindLabel,
    grade: meta.grade,
    clock: clockText(st.time),
    elapsed: timeText(st.time),
    san: st.san,
    money: st.money,
    ev: [foundCount(st), totalEv],
    rec: [Object.keys(st.connected).length, totalRec],
    dlg: [st.talked.length, dlgAll.length],
    ach: [Object.keys(st.achievements).length, totalAch],
    actions: st.stats.actions,
    playMs: Date.now() - st.startedAt,
    pct: {
      ev: totalEv ? foundCount(st) / totalEv : 0,
      rec: totalRec ? Object.keys(st.connected).length / totalRec : 0,
      dlg: dlgAll.length ? st.talked.length / dlgAll.length : 0,
      ach: totalAch ? Object.keys(st.achievements).length / totalAch : 0
    }
  };
}
function gameOver(endingId){
  if (!st || st.ended) return;
  const C = curCase();
  const meta = endingMeta(endingId);
  st.ending = meta;
  st.ended = true;
  st.playMs = Date.now() - st.startedAt;
  checkAchievements();                       /* 先结算成就，报告卡数据才完整（含"真结局"类成就） */
  const report = buildReport(meta);
  st.report = report;
  const isNewGallery = addToGallery(C.id, endingId, meta.grade);
  const newly = recordCaseCompleted(C.id, endingId, {
    isTrue: meta.isTrue, grade: meta.grade, playMs: st.playMs, evidence: foundCount(st)
  });
  st.newArchives = newly.map(x => ({ id:x.id, title:x.title }));
  st.report.isNewGallery = isNewGallery;
  LS.del(KEYS.auto(C.id));                      /* 结案后不再自动续玩 */
  UI.showEnding = true;
  SFX.play(meta.isTrue ? 'unlock' : 'bad');
  renderGame();
  if (newly.length) newly.forEach((x, i) => setTimeout(() => toast('解锁隐藏档案：' + x.id + ' ' + x.title, 'gold', '📂', 5200), 700 + i * 500));
}
function restartCase(){ const id = currentCaseId; const C = CASES[id]; if (!C) return; CAS = C; st = initGameState(C); UI.showEnding = false; UI.combineSlots = [null, null]; goScene(C.initialLocation); }

/* ---------------- 存档 ---------------- */
function saveSnapshot(){ return { v:1, ts:Date.now(), caseId:currentCaseId, title:(CAS && CAS.title) || '', loc:st.loc, ending: st.ended ? (st.ending && st.ending.title) : '', state:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st)) }; }
function saveGame(slot){
  if (!st) return false;
  const ok = LS.set(KEYS.save(currentCaseId, slot), saveSnapshot());
  if (ok) { SFX.play('ok'); toast('已存入存档槽 ' + slot, 'green', '💾'); }
  return ok;
}
function loadGame(slot){
  const raw = LS.get(KEYS.save(currentCaseId, slot), null) || LS.get(KEYS.auto(currentCaseId), null);
  if (!raw || !raw.state) { toast('存档槽 ' + slot + ' 是空的', 'red', '🗂'); return false; }
  if (raw.caseId && raw.caseId !== currentCaseId) { toast('存档与当前案件不符', 'red', '⚠'); return false; }
  st = raw.state;
  st.startedAt = st.startedAt || Date.now();
  st.playMs = st.playMs || 0;
  if (!st.dynActions) st.dynActions = {};
  if (!st.found) st.found = (st.evidence || []).slice();
  UI.showEnding = !!st.ended;
  SFX.play('ok');
  toast('已读取存档（' + new Date(raw.ts).toLocaleString('zh-CN') + '）', 'green', '📂');
  closeGModal();
  renderGame();
  return true;
}
function autosave(){
  if (!st || st.ended) return;
  LS.set(KEYS.auto(currentCaseId), saveSnapshot());
  const note = $('#g-note');
  if (note) { note.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(autosave._t); autosave._t = setTimeout(() => note.classList.remove('show'), 1200); }
}
function slotInfo(caseId, slot){
  const raw = LS.get(KEYS.save(caseId, slot), null);
  if (!raw) return null;
  return { ts: raw.ts, loc: (raw.state && raw.state.loc) || '', ended: !!(raw.state && raw.state.ended),
           time: clockText((raw.state && raw.state.time) || 0), san: (raw.state && raw.state.san) };
}
function autoInfo(caseId){
  const raw = LS.get(KEYS.auto(caseId), null);
  if (!raw) return null;
  return { ts: raw.ts, loc: (raw.state && raw.state.loc) || '', time: clockText((raw.state && raw.state.time) || 0), san: (raw.state && raw.state.san) };
}
function sceneNameOf(caseId, locId){
  const C = CASES[caseId];
  const sc = C && C.sceneData ? C.sceneData[locId] : null;
  return sc ? sc.name : locId;
}
/* ============================== 5.1 引擎界面层 ============================== */
const HEADLESS = !!(typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.__BUREAU_HEADLESS__);   /* 无头测试接缝 */
let gCurrent = null;

function el(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
function toastInGame(text, kind, icon, ms){
  if (HEADLESS) return;
  const root = el('g-pop-root');
  if (!root) return;
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'toast ' + (kind || '');
  d.innerHTML = '<span class="ti" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(icon || 'ℹ') + '</span><span>' + esc(text) + '</span>';
  root.appendChild(d);
  setTimeout(() => { d.classList.add('out'); setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 320); }, ms || 2600);
}
function achievementPop(def){
  if (HEADLESS) return;
  const root = el('g-pop-root');
  if (!root) return;
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'ach-pop';
  d.innerHTML = '<span class="ap-ico" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(def.icon || '🏆') + '</span>' +
    '<div><div class="ap-k">ACHIEVEMENT</div><div class="ap-n">' + esc(def.name) + '</div>' +
    '<div class="ap-d">' + esc(def.desc || '') + '</div></div>';
  root.appendChild(d);
  SFX.play('achieve');
  setTimeout(() => { d.classList.add('out'); setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 400); }, 3600);
}

/* ---------------- 通用游戏弹窗 ---------------- */
function gModal(opt){
  if (HEADLESS) { gCurrent = opt; return; }
  gCurrent = opt;
  renderGModal();
}
function renderGModal(){
  const root = el('g-modal-root');
  if (!root || !gCurrent) return;
  const o = gCurrent;
  root.innerHTML = '<div class="g-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="' + esc(o.title || '对话') + '">' +
    '<div class="g-box ' + (o.wide ? 'wide' : '') + '">' +
      '<div class="gb-head"><h4>' + esc(o.title || '') + '</h4>' +
        '<button class="x-btn" data-g="close" aria-label="关闭">✕</button></div>' +
      '<div class="gb-body">' + (o.body || '') + '</div>' +
      (o.foot === null ? '' : '<div class="gb-foot">' + (o.foot || '<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">关闭</button>') + '</div>') +
    '</div></div>';
  const wrap = root.querySelector('.g-modal');
  const box = root.querySelector('.g-box');
  if (wrap) {
    wrap.onclick = (ev) => {
      if (ev.target === wrap) { closeGModal(); return; }
      const t = ev.target.closest('[data-g]');
      if (!t) return;
      const k = t.getAttribute('data-g');
      if (k === 'close') { closeGModal(); return; }
      if (o.onAction) o.onAction(k, box);
    };
  }
  if (o.onMount && box) o.onMount(box);
  SFX.play('modal');
  const f = box && box.querySelector('input,textarea,.btn');
  if (f) setTimeout(() => { try { f.focus(); } catch (e) {} }, 40);
}
function refreshGModal(body, mount){
  if (HEADLESS || !gCurrent) return;
  gCurrent.body = body;
  if (mount) gCurrent.onMount = mount;
  renderGModal();
}
function closeGModal(){ if (HEADLESS) { gCurrent = null; return; } const root = el('g-modal-root'); if (root) root.innerHTML = ''; gCurrent = null; }
function confirmGModal(title, text, onYes, yesLabel){
  gModal({
    title:title,
    body:'<p style="margin:0;color:var(--ink-2);white-space:pre-wrap">' + esc(text) + '</p>',
    foot:'<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">取消</button><button class="btn danger" data-g="yes">' + esc(yesLabel || '确认') + '</button>',
    onAction:(k) => { if (k === 'yes') { closeGModal(); onYes(); } }
  });
}
function openDoc(key){
  if (HEADLESS) return;
  const C = curCase();
  const d = C && C.docTexts ? C.docTexts[key] : null;
  if (!d) return;
  const text = typeof d === 'string' ? d : d.text;
  const title = typeof d === 'string' ? key : (d.title || key);
  gModal({ title:'档案 · ' + title, body:'<div class="dlg" style="border-left-color:var(--accent)">' + esc(text || '') + '</div>',
    foot:'<button class="btn primary" data-g="close">阅毕</button>' });
}

/* ---------------- 渲染 ---------------- */
function renderTabs(){
  const box = el('g-tabs');
  if (!box) return;
  const tabs = [
    { k:'files', n:'档案', i:'📄' },
    { k:'items', n:'证据', i:'🔎' },
    { k:'tasks', n:'目标', i:'🎯' },
    { k:'codex', n:'图鉴', i:'🏆' }
  ];
  box.innerHTML = tabs.map(t =>
    '<button class="side-tab ' + (UI.tab === t.k ? 'on' : '') + '" role="tab" aria-selected="' + (UI.tab === t.k) + '" data-tab="' + t.k + '">' +
    '<span class="st-i" aria-hidden="true">' + t.i + '</span>' + t.n + '</button>').join('');
  box.onclick = (ev) => {
    const b = ev.target.closest('[data-tab]');
    if (!b) return;
    const k = b.getAttribute('data-tab');
    if (UI.tab === k && isMobile() && el('g-side')) { el('g-side').classList.toggle('collapsed'); return; }
    UI.tab = k; SFX.play('click'); renderSidebar();
    if (el('g-side')) el('g-side').classList.remove('collapsed');
  };
}
function isMobile(){ try { return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width:900px)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }
function bar(p){ return '<div class="mini-bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(clamp(p, 0, 1) * 100) + '%"></i></div>'; }

function renderSidebar(){
  const box = el('g-side-body');
  if (!box) return;
  const C = curCase();
  if (!C) return;
  let h = '';
  if (UI.tab === 'files') {
    const docs = C.docTexts ? Object.keys(C.docTexts) : [];
    h += '<div class="side-panel on"><div class="sp-title">案件简述</div>' +
      '<div class="doc-text" style="font-size:12.8px">' + esc(C.storyIntro || '') + '</div>';
    if (C.storyHint) h += '<div class="doc-text" style="border-left-color:var(--cyan);font-size:12.5px;margin-top:9px">' + esc(C.storyHint) + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">档案文件 ' + st.docs.length + '/' + docs.length + '</div>';
    if (!docs.length) h += '<div class="no-ev">本案无独立档案文件。</div>';
    docs.forEach(k => {
      const d = C.docTexts[k];
      const read = st.docs.indexOf(k) >= 0;
      const title = typeof d === 'string' ? k : (d.title || k);
      h += '<button class="doc-btn ' + (read ? 'read' : '') + '" data-doc="' + esc(k) + '">' +
        '<span class="di" aria-hidden="true">' + (read ? '📖' : '📄') + '</span>' +
        '<span style="flex:1">' + (read ? esc(title) : '未阅档案 ' + esc(title)) + '</span>' +
        (read ? '<span class="chip dim">已阅</span>' : '') + '</button>';
    });
    h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">现场物证 ' + foundCount(st) + '/' + (C.evidenceList || []).length + '</div>' +
      bar((C.evidenceList || []).length ? foundCount(st) / C.evidenceList.length : 0) + '</div>';
  } else if (UI.tab === 'items') {
    h += '<div class="side-panel on"><div class="sp-title">线索关联台</div>' +
      '<div class="btn-row"><button class="btn sm primary" data-gact="combine">打开关联台 (C)</button>' +
      '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-gact="save">存档 (S)</button></div>' +
      '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">持有物证 ' + st.evidence.length + '</div>';
    if (!st.evidence.length) h += '<div class="no-ev">尚未取得物证。现场走一走，能拿的都拿。</div>';
    st.evidence.forEach(name => {
      const desc = (C.itemDesc && C.itemDesc[name]) || '';
      h += '<div class="ev-card"><div class="ev-h"><span class="ev-i" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(itemIcon(name)) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="ev-n">' + esc(name) + '</span></div>' +
        (desc ? '<div class="ev-d">' + esc(desc.slice(0, 60)) + (desc.length > 60 ? '…' : '') + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="ev-a"><button class="btn" data-ev="' + esc(name) + '">使用 / 丢弃</button>' +
        '<button class="btn ghost" data-evc="' + esc(name) + '">放入关联台</button></div></div>';
    });
    if (st.conclusions.length) {
      h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">已成立结论 ' + st.conclusions.length + '</div>';
      st.conclusions.forEach(n => { h += '<div class="ev-card"><div class="ev-h"><span class="ev-i" aria-hidden="true">🔗</span><span class="ev-n">' + esc(n) + '</span></div></div>'; });
    }
    h += '</div>';
  } else if (UI.tab === 'tasks') {
    const mains = C.mainTasks || [], sides = C.sideTasks || [];
    h += '<div class="side-panel on"><div class="sp-title">主线任务</div>';
    if (!mains.length) h += '<div class="no-ev">本案未设置主线任务。</div>';
    mains.forEach(t => {
      const done = checkDef(t); const pr = taskProgress(t);
      h += '<div class="task-card ' + (done ? 'done' : '') + '"><div class="task-h"><span class="tk">主线</span>' + esc(t.name) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="task-c">' + esc(t.desc || '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="task-c">' + bar(done ? 1 : 0) + '<span>' + (done ? '已完成' : '进行中') + '</span></div></div>';
    });
    h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">支线任务</div>';
    if (!sides.length) h += '<div class="no-ev">本案未设置支线任务。</div>';
    sides.forEach(t => {
      const done = checkDef(t); const pr = taskProgress(t);
      h += '<div class="task-card ' + (done ? 'done' : '') + '"><div class="task-h"><span class="tk">支线</span>' + esc(t.name) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="task-c">' + esc(t.desc || '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="task-c">' + bar(pr ? pr[0] / pr[1] : (done ? 1 : 0)) + '<span>' + (pr ? pr[0] + '/' + pr[1] : (done ? '已完成' : '进行中')) + '</span></div></div>';
    });
    h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">调查日志</div>';
    if (!st.log.length) h += '<div class="no-ev">暂无记录。</div>';
    st.log.slice(-8).reverse().forEach(l => {
      h += '<div class="task-c" style="align-items:flex-start"><span class="mono" style="color:var(--ink-4);flex:none">' + esc(l.t) + '</span>' +
        '<span style="flex:1">' + esc(l.text) + '</span></div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
  } else {
    const ach = C.achievements || {};
    const ekeys = C.endings ? Object.keys(C.endings) : [];
    const gal = getGallery(C.id);
    h += '<div class="side-panel on"><div class="sp-title">成就 ' + Object.keys(st.achievements).length + '/' + Object.keys(ach).length + '</div>';
    Object.keys(ach).forEach(id => {
      const a = ach[id]; const got = !!st.achievements[id];
      h += '<div class="ach-card ' + (got ? 'got' : '') + '"><span class="gi" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(a.icon || '🏆') + '</span>' +
        '<div><div class="ach-n">' + esc(a.name) + '</div><div class="ach-d">' + esc(a.desc || '') + '</div></div></div>';
    });
    h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">结局图鉴 ' + Object.keys(gal).length + '/' + ekeys.length + '</div>';
    ekeys.forEach(k => {
      const e = C.endings[k]; const got = !!gal[k];
      h += '<div class="gal-card ' + (got ? (e.true ? 'true' : 'got') : '') + '"><span aria-hidden="true">' + (got ? (e.true ? '★' : '◆') : '◇') + '</span>' +
        '<div><div class="ach-n">' + (got ? esc(e.title) : '？？？') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="ach-d">' + (got ? esc(e.sub || '') : '尚未达成') + '</div></div></div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
  }
  box.innerHTML = h;
  box.onclick = (ev) => {
    const t = ev.target.closest('[data-doc],[data-ev],[data-evc],[data-gact]');
    if (!t) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-doc')) { readDoc(t.getAttribute('data-doc'), false); renderSidebar(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-ev')) { useEvidence(t.getAttribute('data-ev')); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-evc')) { openCombine(t.getAttribute('data-evc')); return; }
    const g = t.getAttribute('data-gact');
    if (g === 'combine') openCombine(null);
    else if (g === 'save') openSaveMenu();
  };
}

function renderSceneView(){
  const inner = el('g-stage-inner');
  if (!inner) return;
  const C = curCase();
  const sc = sceneDef(st.loc);
  if (!sc) { inner.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">场景数据缺失：' + esc(st.loc) + '</div>'; return; }
  let h = '<h1 class="scene-name">' + esc(sc.name) + '</h1>' +
    '<div class="scene-meta">' + esc(C.id) + ' · ' + esc(sc.name) + ' · ' + clockText(st.time) + ' · 到访 ' + (st.sceneVisits[st.loc] || 1) + ' 次</div>' +
    '<div class="scene-desc" id="g-desc"></div>' +
    '<div class="sp-title" style="margin:18px 0 9px">可执行的行动</div>' +
    '<div class="act-list" id="g-acts"></div>';
  inner.innerHTML = h;
  renderActions();
  const desc = sc.desc || '';
  if (getSettings().typewriter && !HEADLESS) typewriter(el('g-desc'), desc);
  else if (el('g-desc')) el('g-desc').textContent = desc;
}
function renderActions(){
  const box = el('g-acts');
  if (!box) return;
  const list = sceneActions(st.loc).filter(actionAvailable);
  let h = '';
  let idx = 0;
  list.forEach(a => {
    const enabled = actionEnabled(a);
    const done = isActionDone(a);
    idx++;
    const key = idx <= 9 ? '<span class="kbd-hint">' + idx + '</span>' : '';
    h += '<button class="act k-' + esc(a.type || 'flag') + ' ' + (done ? 'done' : '') + ' ' + (enabled ? '' : 'locked') + '" ' +
      'data-act-id="' + esc(a.id) + '" data-key="' + idx + '" ' + (enabled ? '' : 'disabled') + '>' +
      '<span class="ai" aria-hidden="true">' + (enabled ? esc(actionIcon(a)) : '🔒') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="at"><span class="al">' + esc(actionLabel(a)) + key + '</span>' +
      (a.hint && getSettings().hints ? '<span class="ah">' + esc(enabled ? a.hint : ('需要前置：' + a.hint)) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="ao">' + esc(enabled ? costText(a) : '未解锁') + '</span></button>';
  });
  if (!h) h = '<div class="no-ev">此处已无可执行的动作。</div>';
  box.innerHTML = h;
  box.onclick = (ev) => {
    const b = ev.target.closest('[data-act-id]');
    if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    doAction(b.getAttribute('data-act-id'));
  };
}
/* 打字机 */
function typewriter(node, text){
  if (!node) return;
  if (twTimer) { clearInterval(twTimer); twTimer = null; }
  const speed = 16;
  let i = 0;
  node.textContent = '';
  const caret = document.createElement('span');
  caret.className = 'tw-caret';
  node.appendChild(caret);
  twTimer = setInterval(() => {
    i += 2;
    if (i >= text.length) {
      clearInterval(twTimer); twTimer = null;
      node.textContent = text;
      if (st && st.startedAt && Date.now() - lastTick > 300 && !HEADLESS) SFX.play('type');
      return;
    }
    caret.remove();
    node.textContent = text.slice(0, i);
    node.appendChild(caret);
    lastTick = Date.now();
  }, speed);
}
function skipTypewriter(){
  const node = el('g-desc');
  if (!node || !twTimer) return false;
  clearInterval(twTimer); twTimer = null;
  node.textContent = (sceneDef(st.loc) || {}).desc || '';
  return true;
}

/* ---------------- 结局与结案报告卡 ---------------- */
function renderEndingView(){
  const inner = el('g-stage-inner');
  if (!inner) return;
  const m = st.ending;
  const r = st.report || buildReport(m);
  const C = curCase();
  const kindCls = m.kind === 'true' ? 't-true' : (m.kind === 'fail' || m.kind === 'death' ? 't-bad' : '');
  let h = '<div class="end-wrap">' +
    '<div class="end-kind">' + esc(m.kindLabel) + ' · ' + esc(C.id) + '</div>' +
    '<h1 class="end-title ' + kindCls + '">' + esc(m.title) + '</h1>' +
    '<div class="end-sub">' + esc(m.sub) + '</div>' +
    '<div class="end-text">' + esc(m.text) + '</div>';
  if (m.epilogue) h += '<div class="epilogue">' + esc(m.epilogue) + '</div>';

  h += '<div class="report">' +
    '<div class="rep-head"><div class="rh">' +
      '<div class="rep-kicker">CASE REPORT · 结案报告卡</div>' +
      '<div class="rep-case">' + esc(C.id) + '　' + esc(C.title) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="rep-sub">' + esc(m.kindLabel) + ' · 结案时刻 ' + clockText(st.time) + ' · 调查耗时 ' + timeText(st.time) + '</div>' +
    '</div><div class="grade g-' + esc(m.grade) + '">' + esc(m.grade) + '<small>RATING</small></div></div>' +
    '<div class="rep-rows">' +
      repRow('结案类型', m.kindLabel) +
      repRow('剩余专注', st.san + ' / 100') +
      repRow('行动次数', r.actions + ' 次') +
      repRow('剩余经费', '¥' + st.money) +
      repRow('现实耗时', fmtDuration(r.playMs)) +
      repRow('结局收录', r.isNewGallery ? '首次收录' : '已收录') +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="rep-bars">' +
      repBar('证据收集', r.ev) + repBar('线索关联', r.rec) + repBar('问询记录', r.dlg) + repBar('成就解锁', r.ach) +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="rep-foot">' + esc(typeof C.summaryEpilogue === 'string' ? C.summaryEpilogue : (C.summaryEpilogue || '')) + '</div>' +
  '</div>';
  if (st.newArchives && st.newArchives.length) {
    h += '<div class="rep-new">📂 解锁隐藏档案：' + st.newArchives.map(a => esc(a.id + ' ' + a.title)).join('、') +
      '　—— 可在档案库中查阅全文。</div>';
  }
  h += '<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:18px">' +
    '<button class="btn primary" data-eact="archive">返回档案库</button>' +
    '<button class="btn" data-eact="restart">重新调查本案</button>' +
    '<button class="btn ghost" data-eact="copy">复制结案报告</button>' +
    '<button class="btn ghost" data-eact="keys">键位说明</button>' +
    '</div></div>';
  inner.innerHTML = h;
  const box = inner.querySelector('.end-wrap');
  if (box) box.onclick = (ev) => {
    const b = ev.target.closest('[data-eact]');
    if (!b) return;
    const k = b.getAttribute('data-eact');
    if (k === 'archive') { exitCase(); go('archives'); }
    else if (k === 'restart') { confirmGModal('重新调查', '本案将从 09:00 重新开始。存档与图鉴不受影响。', () => { closeGModal(); restartCase(); }, '重新开始'); }
    else if (k === 'copy') { copyCaseReport(); }
    else if (k === 'keys') { openKeysHelp(); }
  };
  const stage = el('g-stage');
  if (stage) stage.scrollTop = 0;
}
function repRow(k, v){ return '<div class="rep-row"><span class="rk">' + esc(k) + '</span><span class="rv">' + esc(v) + '</span></div>'; }
function repBar(label, pair){
  const cur = pair[0], tot = pair[1];
  const pct = tot ? cur / tot : 0;
  return '<div class="rb"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><div class="mini-bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(pct * 100) + '%"></i></div>' +
    '<span class="rbv">' + cur + '/' + tot + '</span></div>';
}
function caseReportText(){
  const C = curCase();
  const m = st.ending || { kindLabel:'', grade:'', title:'', sub:'' };
  const r = st.report || buildReport(m);
  return '【调查局档案系统 · 结案报告卡】\n' +
    '案件编号：' + C.id + '　案由：' + C.title + '\n' +
    '结案类型：' + m.kindLabel + '　结局：' + m.title + '\n' +
    '评级：' + m.grade + '　结案时刻：' + clockText(st.time) + '　调查耗时：' + timeText(st.time) + '\n' +
    '剩余专注：' + st.san + '/100　剩余经费：¥' + st.money + '　行动次数：' + r.actions + '\n' +
    '证据收集：' + r.ev[0] + '/' + r.ev[1] + '　线索关联：' + r.rec[0] + '/' + r.rec[1] +
    '　问询记录：' + r.dlg[0] + '/' + r.dlg[1] + '　成就解锁：' + r.ach[0] + '/' + r.ach[1] + '\n' +
    (typeof C.summaryEpilogue === 'string' && C.summaryEpilogue ? C.summaryEpilogue + '\n' : '');
}
function copyCaseReport(){
  const text = caseReportText();
  copyText(text, '结案报告已复制到剪贴板');
}
function copyText(text, okMsg){
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => toast(okMsg || '已复制', 'green', '📋'), () => fallbackCopy(text, okMsg));
      return;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  fallbackCopy(text, okMsg);
}
function fallbackCopy(text, okMsg){
  appModal({ title:'手动复制', body:'<textarea rows="12" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px">' + esc(text) + '</textarea>', foot:'<button class="btn primary" data-act="close">关闭</button>' });
  toast('已为你展开文本，请手动复制', 'gold', '📋');
}

/* ---------------- 存档 / 读档 / 键位 ---------------- */
function openSaveMenu(forCaseId){
  const cid = forCaseId || currentCaseId;
  const C = CASES[cid];
  if (!C) return;
  let h = '<div class="sp-title">自动存档</div>';
  const ai = autoInfo(cid);
  h += '<div class="slot-row"><span class="si">AUTO</span><span class="sm">' +
    (ai ? '<b>' + esc(sceneNameOf(cid, ai.loc)) + ' · ' + esc(ai.time) + ' · 专注 ' + ai.san + '</b>' + new Date(ai.ts).toLocaleString('zh-CN')
        : '<b>暂无自动存档</b>每次场景切换自动写入') + '</span>' +
    (ai ? '<button class="btn sm" data-slot="auto" data-op="load">读取</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-slot="auto" data-op="del">删除</button>' : '') +
    '</div>';
  h += '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:13px">手动存档槽</div>';
  for (let s = 1; s <= 3; s++) {
    const info = slotInfo(cid, s);
    h += '<div class="slot-row"><span class="si">SLOT ' + s + '</span><span class="sm">' +
      (info ? '<b>' + esc(sceneNameOf(cid, info.loc)) + ' · ' + esc(info.time) + ' · 专注 ' + info.san + ' · 评级 ' + (st && st.ending ? (st.ending.grade || '—') : '—') + '</b>' + new Date(info.ts).toLocaleString('zh-CN')
            : '<b>空槽位</b>尚未写入') + '</span>' +
      '<button class="btn sm primary" data-slot="' + s + '" data-op="save">存入</button>' +
      (info ? '<button class="btn sm" data-slot="' + s + '" data-op="load">读取</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-slot="' + s + '" data-op="del">删除</button>' : '') +
      '</div>';
  }
  const body = h + '<div style="margin-top:13px;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-4)">存档保存在本机浏览器（localStorage），与既有版本 key 完全兼容。</div>';
  const inGame = !!st && cid === currentCaseId;
  const doOp = (slot, op) => {
    if (op === 'save') {
      if (!inGame) { toast('请先进入案件再存档', 'red', '⚠'); return; }
      const target = (slot === 'auto') ? null : parseInt(slot, 10);
      if (target) { saveGame(target); closeGModal(); openSaveMenu(cid); }
    } else if (op === 'load') {
      const key = slot === 'auto' ? KEYS.auto(cid) : KEYS.save(cid, slot);
      const raw = LS.get(key, null);
      if (!raw) { toast('该槽位是空的', 'red', '🗂'); return; }
      closeGModal();
      if (!st || cid !== currentCaseId) { startCase(cid, { state: raw.state }); toast('已读取存档', 'green', '📂'); }
      else { st = raw.state; if (!st.found) st.found = (st.evidence || []).slice(); UI.showEnding = !!st.ended; renderGame(); toast('已读取存档', 'green', '📂'); }
    } else if (op === 'del') {
      confirmGModal('删除存档', '确定删除该槽位的存档？此操作不可撤销。', () => {
        if (slot === 'auto') LS.del(KEYS.auto(cid)); else LS.del(KEYS.save(cid, slot));
        closeGModal(); toast('已删除存档', 'green', '🗑'); openSaveMenu(cid);
      }, '删除');
    }
  };
  if (inGame) {
    gModal({ title:'存档 / 读档 · ' + C.id, body:body,
      foot:'<button class="btn ghost" data-g="close">关闭</button>',
      onAction:(k, box) => { if (k === 'close') closeGModal(); },
      onMount:(box) => { box.onclick = (ev) => { const b = ev.target.closest('[data-op]'); if (b) doOp(b.getAttribute('data-slot'), b.getAttribute('data-op')); }; }
    });
  } else {
    appModal({ title:'选择存档 · ' + C.id + ' ' + C.title, wide:true, body:body,
      foot:'<button class="btn" data-act="newgame">开始新调查</button><button class="btn ghost" data-act="close">关闭</button>',
      onAction:(k, box) => {
        if (k === 'newgame') { closeAppModal(); startCase(cid, { fresh:true }); return; }
        const b = box.querySelector('[data-op]');
        if (b) doOp(b.getAttribute('data-slot'), b.getAttribute('data-op'));
      }
    });
    const root = $('#app-modal-root');
    if (root) {
      root.onclick = (ev) => {
        const b = ev.target.closest('[data-op]');
        if (b) { doOp(b.getAttribute('data-slot'), b.getAttribute('data-op')); return; }
        const a = ev.target.closest('[data-act]');
        if (a) { const k = a.getAttribute('data-act'); if (k === 'newgame') { closeAppModal(); startCase(cid, { fresh:true }); } else if (k === 'close') closeAppModal(); return; }
        if (ev.target === root) closeAppModal();
      };
    }
  }
}
function openKeysHelp(){
  const keys = [
    ['1 - 9', '选择对应编号的行动'], ['Tab / Shift+Tab', '切换侧栏分页'],
    ['S', '存档 / 读档'], ['C', '打开线索关联台'], ['M', '跳到档案分页'],
    ['Enter', '跳过打字机动画'], ['?', '本键位说明'], ['Esc', '关闭弹窗 / 最小化引擎']
  ];
  gModal({ title:'键位与操作说明',
    body:'<div class="keys">' + keys.map(k => '<div class="key-row"><kbd>' + esc(k[0]) + '</kbd><span>' + esc(k[1]) + '</span></div>').join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sp-title" style="margin-top:14px">无障碍</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-3)">全部行动均为可聚焦按钮，支持 Tab + 回车操作；弹窗内 Esc 关闭、焦点自动落在首个控件；' +
      '已在「个人信息 → 引擎设置」提供打字机开关，并遵循系统的"减少动态效果"设置。</div>',
    foot:'<button class="btn primary" data-g="close">知道了</button>' });
}

/* ---------------- 案件启动 / 退出 / 最小化 ---------------- */
function startCaseFlow(caseId, mode){
  const C = CASES[caseId];
  if (!C) { toast('卷宗数据缺失：' + caseId, 'red', '⚠'); return; }
  if (mode === 'menu') { openSaveMenu(caseId); return; }
  const auto = autoInfo(caseId);
  const hasSave = [1, 2, 3].some(s => slotInfo(caseId, s));
  if (auto) {
    appModal({
      title:'继续上次调查？',
      body:'<p style="margin:0 0 10px;color:var(--ink-2)">检测到 <b>' + esc(C.id) + ' ' + esc(C.title) + '</b> 的自动存档：</p>' +
        '<div class="slot-row"><span class="si">AUTO</span><span class="sm"><b>' + esc(sceneNameOf(caseId, auto.loc)) + ' · ' + esc(auto.time) + ' · 专注 ' + auto.san + '</b>' +
        new Date(auto.ts).toLocaleString('zh-CN') + '</span></div>',
      foot:'<button class="btn primary" data-act="resume">继续调查</button>' +
           '<button class="btn" data-act="menu">读取存档</button>' +
           '<button class="btn ghost" data-act="new">重新开始</button>',
      onAction:(k) => {
        if (k === 'resume') { const raw = LS.get(KEYS.auto(caseId), null); closeAppModal(); if (raw) startCase(caseId, { state: raw.state }); }
        else if (k === 'menu') { closeAppModal(); openSaveMenu(caseId); }
        else if (k === 'new') { closeAppModal(); confirmModal('重新开始', '将从 09:00 重新开始本案，自动存档会被覆盖（手动存档不受影响）。', () => startCase(caseId, { fresh:true }), '重新开始'); }
      }
    });
    return;
  }
  if (hasSave) { openSaveMenu(caseId); return; }
  startCase(caseId, { fresh:true });
}
function startCase(caseId, opts){
  const C = CASES[caseId];
  if (!C) { toast('卷宗数据缺失：' + caseId, 'red', '⚠'); return; }
  const o = opts || {};
  currentCaseId = caseId;
  CAS = C;
  st = o.state ? o.state : initGameState(C);
  if (!st.dynActions) st.dynActions = {};
  if (!st.found) st.found = (st.evidence || []).slice();
  UI.showEnding = !!st.ended;
  UI.combineSlots = [null, null];
  if (UI.showEnding && st.ending) st.ending = typeof st.ending === 'string' ? endingMeta(st.ending) : st.ending;
  saveSettings({ lastCase: caseId });
  const host = el('game-host');
  if (host) { host.classList.add('open'); host.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false'); }
  const side = el('g-side');
  if (side) side.classList.toggle('collapsed', isMobile());   /* 移动端：先让场景占满屏幕 */
  document.body.classList.add('playing');
  UI.gameOpen = true;
  closeDetail();
  closeGModal();
  goScene(st.loc);
  if (host) host.focus();
  if (!HEADLESS) toast('进入 ' + C.id + ' · ' + C.title, 'gold', '🗂', 2400);
}
function minimizeCase(){
  const host = el('game-host');
  if (host) { host.classList.remove('open'); host.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); }
  UI.gameOpen = false;
  updatePlaybar();
  toast('引擎已最小化，进度保留在自动存档中', 'gold', '🗕');
}
function resumeCase(){
  if (!st) return;
  const host = el('game-host');
  if (host) { host.classList.add('open'); host.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false'); }
  UI.gameOpen = true;
  renderGame();
}
function exitCase(){
  if (st && !st.ended && getSettings().autosave) autosave();
  const host = el('game-host');
  if (host) { host.classList.remove('open'); host.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); }
  document.body.classList.remove('playing');
  UI.gameOpen = false;
  UI.showEnding = false;
  if (twTimer) { clearInterval(twTimer); twTimer = null; }
  closeGModal();
  st = null; CAS = null; currentCaseId = '';
  render();
}
function updatePlaybar(){
  const t = el('pb-text');
  if (!t) return;
  if (!st || !CAS) { t.textContent = '案件进行中'; return; }
  t.textContent = CAS.id + ' · ' + ((sceneDef(st.loc) || {}).name || '') + ' · ' + clockText(st.time) + ' · 专注 ' + st.san;
}
function renderGame(){
  if (HEADLESS || !st) return;
  const C = curCase();
  if (!C) return;
  if (el('g-case-id')) el('g-case-id').textContent = C.id;
  if (el('g-case-title')) el('g-case-title').textContent = C.title;
  if (el('g-loc-txt')) el('g-loc-txt').textContent = ((sceneDef(st.loc) || {}).name || st.loc);
  if (el('g-time')) el('g-time').textContent = clockText(st.time) + ' / ' + clockText(C.timeLimit || 540);
  if (el('g-san')) el('g-san').textContent = st.san;
  if (el('g-money')) el('g-money').textContent = st.money;
  const fill = el('g-san-fill');
  if (fill) {
    fill.style.width = st.san + '%';
    fill.className = 'san-fill' + (st.san <= 20 ? ' crit' : st.san <= 45 ? ' low' : '');
  }
  renderTabs();
  renderSidebar();
  if (UI.showEnding && st.ending) renderEndingView(); else renderSceneView();
  updatePlaybar();
}
/* ---------------- 键盘 ---------------- */
function onKeyDown(ev){
  const tag = (ev.target && ev.target.tagName) || '';
  const typing = tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT' || (ev.target && ev.target.isContentEditable);
  if (ev.key === 'Escape') {
    if (el('g-modal-root') && el('g-modal-root').innerHTML) { closeGModal(); ev.preventDefault(); return; }
    if (!$('#app-modal-root').classList.contains('hidden')) { closeAppModal(); ev.preventDefault(); return; }
    if (UI.gameOpen) { minimizeCase(); ev.preventDefault(); }
    return;
  }
  if (typing) return;
  if (!document.body.classList.contains('playing') || !st) return;
  if (ev.key === 'Enter') { if (skipTypewriter()) { ev.preventDefault(); } return; }
  if (ev.key === '?' || (ev.key === '/' && ev.shiftKey)) { openKeysHelp(); ev.preventDefault(); return; }
  if (ev.key === 'Tab') {
    const order = ['files', 'items', 'tasks', 'codex'];
    let i = order.indexOf(UI.tab);
    i = (i + (ev.shiftKey ? -1 : 1) + order.length) % order.length;
    UI.tab = order[i]; renderSidebar(); renderTabs(); ev.preventDefault(); return;
  }
  const k = ev.key.toLowerCase();
  if (k === 's' || k === 'l') { openSaveMenu(); ev.preventDefault(); return; }
  if (k === 'c') { openCombine(null); ev.preventDefault(); return; }
  if (k === 'm') { UI.tab = 'files'; renderSidebar(); renderTabs(); ev.preventDefault(); return; }
  if (/^[1-9]$/.test(ev.key)) {
    const b = document.querySelector('#g-acts [data-key="' + ev.key + '"]');
    if (b && !b.disabled) { b.focus(); doAction(b.getAttribute('data-act-id')); ev.preventDefault(); }
  }
}

/* ---------------- 案件工坊（JSON 导入 / 导出 / 校验） ---------------- */
const KNOWN_TYPES = ['goto','pick','doc','talk','press','combine','use','drop','flag','rest','verdict'];
function normalizeCase(o){
  const c = Object.assign({}, o);
  c.id = String(c.id || '').trim();
  c.title = c.title || '未命名案件';
  c.subtitle = c.subtitle || '';
  c.year = c.year || '——'; c.place = c.place || '未知';
  c.initialSan = c.initialSan == null ? 100 : c.initialSan;
  c.initialTime = c.initialTime || 0;
  c.initialMoney = c.initialMoney || 0;
  c.timeLimit = c.timeLimit || 540;
  c.evidenceList = Array.isArray(c.evidenceList) ? c.evidenceList : [];
  c.docTexts = c.docTexts || {};
  c.itemDesc = c.itemDesc || {};
  c.itemIcons = c.itemIcons || {};
  c.sceneData = c.sceneData || {};
  c.npcData = c.npcData || {};
  c.recipes = Array.isArray(c.recipes) ? c.recipes : [];
  c.achievements = c.achievements || {};
  c.mainTasks = Array.isArray(c.mainTasks) ? c.mainTasks : [];
  c.sideTasks = Array.isArray(c.sideTasks) ? c.sideTasks : [];
  c.endings = c.endings || {};
  c.storyIntro = c.storyIntro || '';
  c.storyHint = c.storyHint || '';
  if (!c.initialLocation || !c.sceneData[c.initialLocation]) c.initialLocation = Object.keys(c.sceneData)[0] || '';
  return c;
}
function validateCase(o){
  const errors = [], warns = [];
  if (!o || typeof o !== 'object') return { ok:false, errors:['JSON 根节点必须是对象'], warns:[] };
  const c = normalizeCase(o);
  if (!c.id) errors.push('缺少 id（案件编号，建议形如 A-2001）');
  if (!c.initialLocation) errors.push('缺少可用的 initialLocation（起点场景）');
  if (!Object.keys(c.sceneData).length) errors.push('sceneData 为空');
  Object.keys(c.sceneData).forEach(sid => {
    const sc = c.sceneData[sid];
    if (!sc || typeof sc !== 'object') { errors.push('场景 ' + sid + ' 定义无效'); return; }
    if (!sc.name) warns.push('场景 ' + sid + ' 缺少 name');
    if (!sc.desc) warns.push('场景 ' + sid + ' 缺少 desc');
    const ids = {};
    (sc.actions || []).forEach(a => {
      if (!a || !a.id) { errors.push('场景 ' + sid + ' 存在缺少 id 的行动'); return; }
      if (ids[a.id]) errors.push('场景 ' + sid + ' 行动 id 重复：' + a.id);
      ids[a.id] = 1;
      if (!a.label) warns.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 缺少 label');
      if (a.type && KNOWN_TYPES.indexOf(a.type) < 0) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的类型未知：' + a.type);
      if (a.type === 'goto' && !c.sceneData[a.target]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 指向不存在的场景：' + a.target);
      if (a.type === 'pick' && a.item && c.evidenceList.indexOf(a.item) < 0) warns.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 拾取的物品未列入 evidenceList：' + a.item);
      if (a.type === 'pick' && !a.item) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 是 pick 但没有 item');
      if (a.doc && !c.docTexts[a.doc]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 引用了不存在的文档：' + a.doc);
      if ((a.type === 'talk' || a.type === 'press') && !c.npcData[a.target]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 引用了不存在的人物：' + a.target);
      if (a.type === 'verdict'){
        (a.verdicts || []).forEach(v => { if (!c.endings[v.ending]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的判定指向不存在的结局：' + v.ending); });
        if (a.fallback && !c.endings[a.fallback]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的 fallback 结局不存在：' + a.fallback);
      }
      (a.effects || []).forEach(e => {
        if (e.type === 'doc' && !c.docTexts[e.key]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的效果引用了不存在的文档：' + e.key);
        if (e.type === 'scene' && !c.sceneData[e.target]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的效果指向不存在的场景：' + e.target);
        if (e.type === 'ending' && !c.endings[e.id]) errors.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的效果指向不存在的结局：' + e.id);
        if (e.type === 'item' && c.evidenceList.length && e.item && c.evidenceList.indexOf(e.item) < 0) warns.push('行动 ' + sid + '.' + a.id + ' 的效果给出未登记的物品：' + e.item);
      });
    });
  });
  Object.keys(c.npcData).forEach(nid => {
    const n = c.npcData[nid];
    if (!n.name) warns.push('人物 ' + nid + ' 缺少 name');
    (n.dialogues || []).forEach(d => {
      if (!d.id) errors.push('人物 ' + nid + ' 存在缺少 id 的对话');
      if (!d.label) warns.push('对话 ' + nid + '.' + d.id + ' 缺少 label');
      if (!d.text) warns.push('对话 ' + nid + '.' + d.id + ' 缺少 text');
      (d.effects || []).forEach(e => { if (e.type === 'doc' && !c.docTexts[e.key]) errors.push('对话 ' + nid + '.' + d.id + ' 引用不存在的文档：' + e.key); });
    });
  });
  c.recipes.forEach(r => {
    if (!r.id) errors.push('存在缺少 id 的配方');
    if (!r.a || !r.b || !r.result) errors.push('配方 ' + (r.id || '?') + ' 缺少 a/b/result');
  });
  if (!Object.keys(c.endings).length) errors.push('endings 为空：至少需要一个结局');
  Object.keys(c.endings).forEach(k => {
    if (!c.endings[k].title) warns.push('结局 ' + k + ' 缺少 title');
    if (!c.endings[k].text) warns.push('结局 ' + k + ' 缺少 text');
  });
  /* 可达性 */
  if (c.initialLocation) {
    const seen = {}, queue = [c.initialLocation];
    seen[c.initialLocation] = 1;
    while (queue.length) {
      const cur = queue.shift();
      const sc = c.sceneData[cur];
      if (!sc) continue;
      (sc.actions || []).forEach(a => {
        if (a.type === 'goto' && a.target && c.sceneData[a.target] && !seen[a.target]) { seen[a.target] = 1; queue.push(a.target); }
      });
    }
    Object.keys(c.sceneData).forEach(sid => { if (!seen[sid]) warns.push('场景 ' + sid + ' 无法从起点到达（检查 goto 行动）'); });
  }
  return { ok:errors.length === 0, errors:errors, warns:warns, normalized:c };
}
function workshopTemplate(){
  return {
    id:'A-2001', title:'示范案件：空房间的第三把椅子', subtitle:'由案件工坊导入的示例卷宗',
    year:'2001', place:'某处', initialLocation:'room', initialSan:100, initialTime:0, initialMoney:50, timeLimit:540,
    storyIntro:'一间空房间，两把椅子，和第三把刚被人坐过的椅子。\n这是一份最小可玩案件模板：改掉它，就能写你自己的案子。',
    storyHint:'试试拾取物证，然后在关联台里把两件物证放在一起。',
    evidenceList:['磨损的椅腿','半张便签'], itemIcons:{ '磨损的椅腿':'🪑','半张便签':'📝' },
    itemDesc:{ '磨损的椅腿':'椅腿内侧有新鲜的刮痕。','半张便签':'只有半张，写着半句话。' },
    docTexts:{ d1:{ title:'房间勘查记录', text:'房间约 12 平方米，两把旧椅子靠墙，第三把椅子在房间正中。\n地面无脚印，窗户自内侧锁闭。' } },
    sceneData:{
      room:{
        name:'空房间', desc:'房间正中摆着第三把椅子，椅面还有余温。两把旧椅子靠墙，落满灰。',
        actions:[
          { id:'t_see', label:'查看现场并阅读勘查记录', type:'flag', cost:5, effects:[{ type:'doc', key:'d1' }, { type:'flag', key:'seen' }] },
          { id:'t_pick1', label:'拾起磨损的椅腿', type:'pick', item:'磨损的椅腿', cost:5, once:true },
          { id:'t_pick2', label:'拾起地上的半张便签', type:'pick', item:'半张便签', cost:5, once:true },
          { id:'t_rest', label:'在旧椅子上坐一会儿', type:'rest', cost:15, san:6 },
          { id:'t_report', label:'提交结案报告', type:'verdict', cost:10, hint:'依现有证据结案',
            verdicts:[{ ending:'true', cond:{ combine:'R1' } }], fallback:'cold' }
        ]
      }
    },
    npcData:{},
    recipes:[{ id:'R1', a:'磨损的椅腿', b:'半张便签', result:'第三把椅子是被人搬进来的',
      desc:'椅腿磨损与地面刮痕方向一致，便签上的字迹说明有人在这里等了很久。', effect:[{ type:'flag', key:'r1' }] }],
    achievements:{ a1:{ icon:'🪑', name:'三把椅子', desc:'建立唯一的线索关联', when:{ combine:'R1' } } },
    mainTasks:[{ id:'M1', name:'查清第三把椅子的来历', desc:'房间本来只有两把椅子。', when:{ combine:'R1' } }],
    sideTasks:[],
    endings:{
      true:{ title:'第三把椅子', sub:'有人在这里等了很久', kind:'true', kindLabel:'真结局',
        text:'你把椅腿的磨损与便签的折痕对在一起，得出一个平静而难过的结论：第三把椅子是被人从外面搬进来的。\n他在这里等了很久，然后离开了。', epilogue:'房间后来被清空，椅子被送去了仓库。' },
      cold:{ title:'空房间', sub:'证据不足', kind:'cold', kindLabel:'悬案', text:'两把旧椅子，一把新椅子。你没能把这条线连起来。', epilogue:'' }
    },
    summaryEpilogue:'这个案件由案件工坊的示例模板生成——换成你自己的故事吧。'
  };
}
function workshopImport(){
  const ta = $('#ws-json');
  const msg = $('#ws-msg');
  if (!ta) return;
  let obj = null;
  try { obj = JSON.parse(ta.value); }
  catch (e) { if (msg) msg.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef9b9a">JSON 解析失败：' + esc(e.message) + '</span>'; SFX.play('bad'); return; }
  const res = validateCase(obj);
  if (!res.ok) {
    SFX.play('bad');
    if (msg) msg.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef9b9a">校验未通过（' + res.errors.length + ' 处错误）：</span><ul style="margin:6px 0 0 18px;color:var(--ink-3)">' +
      res.errors.slice(0, 12).map(e => '<li>' + esc(e) + '</li>').join('') + '</ul>' +
      (res.warns.length ? '<div style="margin-top:6px;color:var(--ink-4)">另有 ' + res.warns.length + ' 条提示。</div>' : '');
    return;
  }
  saveCustomCase(res.normalized);
  SFX.play('unlock');
  if (msg) msg.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--ok)">导入成功：' + esc(res.normalized.id) + ' ' + esc(res.normalized.title) + '</span>' +
    (res.warns.length ? '<div style="margin-top:6px;color:var(--ink-4)">' + res.warns.slice(0, 6).map(esc).join('<br>') + '</div>' : '');
  toast('卷宗已导入，可在档案库中开始调查', 'gold', '🧩', 3600);
  render();
}
function workshopExport(){
  const out = {};
  Object.keys(CASES).forEach(k => { try { out[k] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CASES[k])); } catch (e) {} });
  download('bureau-cases-' + todayStr(new Date()) + '.json', JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
}
function exportProgress(){
  const payload = { v:1, exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(), profile:getProfile(), settings:getSettings(),
    progress:getGlobalProgress(), saves:{}, gallery:{} };
  allArchives().filter(a => a.type === 'case').forEach(a => {
    payload.gallery[a.caseId] = getGallery(a.caseId);
    for (let s = 1; s <= 3; s++) { const v = LS.get(KEYS.save(a.caseId, s), null); if (v) payload.saves[a.caseId + '_' + s] = v; }
    const au = LS.get(KEYS.auto(a.caseId), null); if (au) payload.saves[a.caseId + '_auto'] = au;
  });
  download('bureau-progress-' + todayStr(new Date()) + '.json', JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
}
function importProgress(){
  appModal({
    title:'导入进度 JSON', wide:true,
    body:'<p style="margin:0 0 10px;color:var(--ink-3);font-size:13px">粘贴由「导出全部进度」生成的 JSON。同名存档将被覆盖。</p>' +
      '<textarea id="imp-json" rows="10" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px"></textarea><div id="imp-msg" style="margin-top:9px;font-size:12.5px"></div>',
    foot:'<button class="btn primary" data-act="do-import">导入</button><button class="btn ghost" data-act="close">取消</button>',
    onAction:(k, box) => {
      if (k === 'close') { closeAppModal(); return; }
      if (k === 'do-import') {
        const msg = $('#imp-msg');
        let obj = null;
        try { obj = JSON.parse($('#imp-json').value); } catch (e) { if (msg) msg.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef9b9a">JSON 解析失败</span>'; return; }
        if (obj.profile) LS.set(KEYS.profile, Object.assign(DEFAULT_PROFILE, obj.profile));
        if (obj.settings) LS.set(KEYS.settings, Object.assign(DEFAULT_SETTINGS, obj.settings));
        if (obj.progress) LS.set(KEYS.progress, Object.assign(DEFAULT_PROGRESS, obj.progress));
        let n = 0;
        const keys = Object.keys(obj.saves || {});
        keys.forEach(k => {
          const m = k.match(/^(.+)_(1|2|3|auto)$/);
          if (!m) return;
          const caseId = m[1], slot = m[2];
          LS.set(slot === 'auto' ? KEYS.auto(caseId) : KEYS.save(caseId, slot), obj.saves[k]); n++;
        });
        const gk = Object.keys(obj.gallery || {});
        gk.forEach(cid => { LS.set(KEYS.gallery(cid), obj.gallery[cid]); });
        toast('导入完成：' + n + ' 份存档 · ' + gk.length + ' 份图鉴', 'green', '📥', 3600);
        closeAppModal(); render();
      }
    }
  });
}
function copyReport(){
  const prog = getGlobalProgress();
  const cases = allArchives().filter(a => a.type === 'case');
  let t = '【调查局档案系统 · 归档报告】\n生成时间：' + new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN') + '\n\n';
  cases.forEach(a => {
    const r = prog.records[a.caseId];
    const C = CASES[a.caseId];
    const tot = C && C.endings ? Object.keys(C.endings).length : 0;
    t += a.id + ' ' + a.title + '：' + (r ? ('结案 ' + r.completed + ' 次' + (r.trueEnded ? '，真结局已达成' : '')) : '未结案') +
      '，图鉴 ' + Object.keys(getGallery(a.caseId)).length + '/' + tot + '\n';
  });
  t += '\n累计游玩：' + fmtDuration(prog.playMs) + '　累计证据：' + (prog.evidenceTotal || 0) + ' 件\n';
  t += '隐藏档案解锁：' + allArchives().filter(a => a.hidden && isArchiveUnlocked(a, prog)).map(a => a.id).join('、') + '\n';
  copyText(t, '归档报告已复制到剪贴板');
}
function download(name, text){
  try {
    const blob = new Blob([text], { type:'application/json;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = name;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
    setTimeout(() => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); a.remove(); }, 800);
    toast('已导出：' + name, 'green', '⬇');
  } catch (e) {
    appModal({ title:'导出失败，请手动复制', wide:true, body:'<textarea rows="12" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px">' + esc(text) + '</textarea>' });
  }
}

/* ============================== 6. 启动 ============================== */
function boot(){
  loadCustomCases();
  const s = getSettings();
  SFX.on = !!s.sfx;
  state.route = routeFromHash();
  render();

  const nav = $('#nav');
  if (nav) nav.setAttribute('role', 'tablist');

  const host = $('#game-host');
  if (host) {
    host.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
      if (ev.target === host) return;
    });
  }
  const bind = (id, fn) => { const e = el(id); if (e) e.onclick = fn; };
  bind('g-btn-save', () => openSaveMenu());
  bind('g-btn-keys', () => openKeysHelp());
  bind('g-btn-min', () => minimizeCase());
  bind('pb-resume', () => resumeCase());
  bind('pb-save', () => { if (st) openSaveMenu(); else toast('当前没有进行中的案件', 'red', '🗂'); });
  bind('pb-exit', () => confirmModal('退出案件', '将结束本次调查。自动存档已保留，可随时继续。', () => exitCase(), '退出'));

  window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => {
    const r = routeFromHash();
    if (r !== state.route) { state.route = r; state.detail = null; render(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown, false);
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => { if (st && !st.ended && getSettings().autosave) autosave(); });

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (state.route === 'home') toast('档案终端已就绪 · ' + CASE_ARCHIVES.length + ' 份在册卷宗', 'gold', '🗄', 3400);
  }, 600);
}

/* 对外测试 / 调试接口（不影响正常使用） */
window.__BUREAU__ = {
  version: '1.5',
  CASES: CASES,
  ARCHIVES: ARCHIVES,
  allArchives: allArchives,
  getGlobalProgress: getGlobalProgress,
  saveGlobalProgress: saveGlobalProgress,
  recordCaseCompleted: recordCaseCompleted,
  isArchiveUnlocked: isArchiveUnlocked,
  caseStatus: caseStatus,
  getGallery: getGallery,
  initGameState: initGameState,
  condMet: condMet,
  applyEffects: applyEffects,
  doAction: doAction,
  runAction: runAction,
  goScene: goScene,
  sceneActions: sceneActions,
  actionEnabled: actionEnabled,
  actionAvailable: actionAvailable,
  tryCombine: tryCombine,
  askDialogue: askDialogue,
  evaluateVerdict: evaluateVerdict,
  gameOver: gameOver,
  endingMeta: endingMeta,
  buildReport: buildReport,
  clockText: clockText,
  saveGame: saveGame,
  loadGame: loadGame,
  saveSnapshot: saveSnapshot,
  validateCase: validateCase,
  normalizeCase: normalizeCase,
  workshopTemplate: workshopTemplate,
  GRADE_MAP: GRADE_MAP,
  KEYS: KEYS,
  setCase: function(id, opts){
    const C = CASES[id];
    if (!C) return null;
    currentCaseId = id; CAS = C; st = (opts && opts.state) || initGameState(C);
    if (!st.dynActions) st.dynActions = {};
    if (!st.found) st.found = (st.evidence || []).slice();
    UI.showEnding = false;
    return st;
  },
  get state(){ return st; },
  get caseId(){ return currentCaseId; },
  sceneDef: sceneDef,
  npcDef: npcDef,
  dialogueList: function(npcId){
    const n = npcDef(npcId);
    if (!n) return [];
    return (n.dialogues || []).map(d => ({ id:d.id, label:d.label, press: !!d.press, cond: d.cond || null, once: !!d.once }));
  }
};

if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
}
})();

</script>
</body>
</html>
